- **Form theme** `templates/admin/form/theme.html.twig` for Symfony Forms: labels, help, errors linked by `aria-describedby`, required markers, a character counter for limited fields, the locale tabs of S12.
- **Global search** (`SearchProvider`, ⌘K / Ctrl+K command palette island) with a JSON endpoint `/admin/api/search?q=` that merges providers; #24 and #27 add providers.
- **Styleguide** at `/admin/_styleguide` (dev and test only, logged in), one section per component with every variant, like #1's.
- **Done when:**
  - the styleguide matches the design reference at 1440 px;
  - Twig and Vue variants of the same component render identical markup (a Vitest snapshot vs. the Twig output fixture for button, badge, table row and modal);
  - axe/Lighthouse Accessibility ≥ 95 on the styleguide;
  - keyboard walkthrough of combobox, modal, sortable list and command palette;
  - ADM-8 CSS budget.

**Outcome (2026-10-30).** Done in commit `admin-ui-kit`. No migration; Composer `league/commonmark ^2.10.2` (brings `league/config`, `nette/schema`, `nette/utils`, `dflydev/dot-access-data`); no npm additions. Every `/admin` page reads the Vite manifest, so a checkout or deploy needs `make admin-install admin-build` before the first admin request. Every component with every variant: `/admin/_styleguide` (`templates/admin/styleguide/_components.html.twig`, one `<section data-component>` each).

- **Twig kit** (`templates/admin/components/`; each file opens with a docblock of its params; strings arrive translated). Atoms are included `with_context = false`, shells embedded `… only`:
  - `_button` (`label`, `variant` primary|secondary|ghost|danger, `size` sm|xs|lg, `icon`, `icon_only` → `aria-label`, `href` → `<a>`, `type`, `attributes` map); `_pill` (`label`, `tone` green|grey|pink|blue|amber|red|dark, `dot`); `_tier_badge` (`tier` = a `Venues\ValueObject\Tier` value, `label`; a Twig hash, so Admin stays free of Venues, `TierBadgeTest` pins it to the enum); `_empty` (`icon`, `title`, `text`); `_filter_chip` (`label`, `remove_href`, `remove_label`).
  - `_tabs` (status tabs as links: `label`, `items` list<{label, href, count?, tone?, active?}>); `_record_tabs` (`label`, `tabs` from `admin_record_tabs()`, `current_route`); `_locale_tabs` (`id`, `label`, `locales` list<{code, label}>, `panels` map code → markup; a tablist from two locales via `js/admin/tabs.js`, a plain label for one, all panels without JS); `_pagination` (`page`, `last_page`, `total`, `per_page`, `label`, `previous_label`, `next_label`; numbers from `admin_page_links()`, URLs keep the query).
  - `_record_header` (`title`, `subtitle`; blocks `meta`, `actions`, `tabs`); `_filter_bar` (GET form with a visible "Filtern" button: `action`, `id`, `search_name`, `search_value`, `search_label`, `search_placeholder`, `submit_label`, `reset_href`, `reset_label`, `chips`; block `filters`); `_table` (`label`, `id`, `columns` list<{label, sort_key?}>, `sort`, `dir`, `select_label` (adds the selection column), `actions_label` (adds the actions column); blocks `rows`, `empty`, `foot`; a sort header is a link `sort=<key>&dir=asc|desc` with `aria-sort`); `_table_row` (`select_name`, `select_value`, `select_label`, `actions_column`; blocks `cells`, `actions`); `_dropdown` (a `popover`, not `role="menu"`: `id`, `label`, `icon`, `icon_only`, `variant`; block `items`); `_card` (`title`, `id`; blocks `actions`, `body`, `foot`); `_modal`/`_drawer` (`<dialog data-dialog>`: `id`, `title`, `close_label`, `danger`; blocks `body`, `footer`; a destructive confirm gives Cancel `autofocus` through `_button`'s `attributes`).
  - Also `_avatar`, `_skeleton`, `_kpi`, `_progress` (native `<progress>`), `_property`, `_chip_check`, `_seg`, `_timeline_item`, `_kanban_column`, `_kanban_card`, `_dropzone` (a real file input over the zone), `_sortable` (POST move-up/down forms, `aria-disabled` at the ends), `_required_hint`.
  - **`_scripts.html.twig`**, included by `templates/admin/base.html.twig`, loads `js/dialog.js` and `js/admin/{palette-trigger,tabs,dropdown,char-counter,markdown-preview}.js` on every back-office page. **A new admin script goes there** (and is then counted by `AdminAssetBudgetTest`'s glob of `assets/js/admin/*.js`); the scripts delegate at document level, so they work on island markup too. They load from `'self'` and need no nonce under #16's policy.
  - Styles: one `assets/styles/admin/_<component>.scss` each, appended to `admin.scss`; input borders use the new token `--a-field-strong` (≥ 3:1, `AdminInputContrastTest`). Seven icons added to `assets/icons/admin-sprite.svg` (21 → 28).
- **Form theme** `templates/admin/form/theme.html.twig`, opted in per template with `{% form_theme form 'admin/form/theme.html.twig' %}` (never `twig.form_themes`, so the public inquiry form cannot inherit it; `FormThemeTest`). `aria-describedby` joins help, counter, error; `required` shows `*` (add `_required_hint`); `attr: {'data-counter-max': n}` adds a counter; money fields need `html5: true` and show `.field__addon` CHF. #22's `templates/admin/form/_field.html.twig` is deleted; users, audit and TOTP screens use the theme. Form types `App\Admin\Form\ComboboxType` (`url`, `static_options`, `display_value`, `free_text`; select mode submits a hidden value, free-text mode submits the visible input as typed; the plain input is the island's fallback, so it works before the mount and without JS; a `url` answers `{"options": [{"value", "label", "detail"}]}`) and `App\Admin\Form\MarkdownType` (Schreiben/Vorschau tabs).
- **Twig functions:** `admin_query_path(changes)` and `admin_page_links(page, last)` (`App\Admin\Twig\AdminUrlExtension`; the current route and query of the main request with `changes` merged, null removes a key, `page` dropped unless named). `App\Admin\Twig\IslandExtension`: `admin_island(name, props, fallback = '')` (the fallback sits unescaped in the mount point) and new `admin_island_deferred(name, props)` (`data-island-defer`, loads nothing until first use).
- **Vue kit** (`admin-ui/src/`, imported only by islands): `components/Ui{Icon,Button,Pill,Avatar,Empty,Skeleton,TableRow,Modal,Drawer,KanbanColumn,KanbanCard,TimelineItem,Dropzone,SortableList,Combobox}.vue` with the Twig twins' markup; `composables/useToast.js`, `useListbox.js`; `api.js` with `adminFetch(url, {method, body, signal})` (a redirect, 401 or 2xx non-JSON throws `SessionGone`; 204 gives `null`; 422 JSON throws `ValidationFailed` with `.errors`; any other status `RequestFailed` with `.status`). Islands: `command-palette`, `combobox`, `styleguide-sortable`. Twig/Vue parity for button, pill, table row (with and without actions) and modal is pinned by `admin-ui/tests/fixtures/markup/*.html`, checked by `ComponentMarkupFixturesTest` and `markup-parity.test.js`.
- **Global search:** `App\Admin\Extension\SearchProvider` now also has `label(): TranslatableInterface`, `priority()`, `requiredRole()`. `App\Admin\Extension\SearchProviderRegistry::search($q)` asks each visible provider for ≤ 5 results and drops empty groups (`App\Admin\ValueObject\SearchGroup`). `admin_api_search` `GET /admin/api/search?q=` (`App\Admin\Controller\SearchController`, editor, no CSRF on a read) → `{"groups": [{"label", "results": [{"type", "label", "detail", "url"}]}]}`; `q` trimmed, cut to 100 characters, fewer than 2 gives no groups. The sidebar slot `templates/admin/layout/_search_trigger.html.twig` holds the button and the deferred palette; `palette-trigger.js` imports the entry on first click or ⌘K/Ctrl+K and dispatches `admin:mount-islands`. #24 and #27 add providers.
- **Markdown:** `App\Shared\Markdown\MarkdownRenderer::render()` (league/commonmark, core only, raw HTML escaped, unsafe links dropped, nesting and delimiter limits) behind `admin_api_markdown_preview` `POST /admin/api/markdown-preview` (`App\Admin\Controller\MarkdownPreviewController`, `{"text"}` ≤ 20000 → `{"html"}`, a null or non-string text is a 422). Both endpoints depart from `/admin/api/<module>/…` because they belong to no module.
- **Other:** `PageLinks` moved to `App\Shared\ValueObject\PageLinks` (Search's listing uses it from there). The route walker (`tests/Application/Admin/ChecksAdminRoutes.php`) now accepts GET JSON routes (a read answers 200, not the 403 a write without token gets). The kit's strings are an appended `ui:` block in the `admin` catalogue (`ui.<component>.<name>`). `PublicPagesLoadNoAdminAssetsTest` proves public pages load no admin asset.
- **Budgets (ADM-8, `AdminAssetBudgetTest`, gzip -9):** shared JS 29.3 KB of 60 (entry closure 24.9 KB + vanilla scripts 4.4 KB; a chunk shared between islands now counts against each), largest island 3.5 KB of 30 (command palette), admin CSS 9.3 KB of 30. Lighthouse Accessibility 100 on the styleguide; vnu no errors.
- **Deviations:**
  - Design: darker input borders (`--a-field-strong`, WCAG 1.4.11), `*` required marker plus a hint line, an explicit "Filtern" button, 24 px chip remove button, no bell, global "Neu" moves to #24; modal, drawer, dropdown, skeleton, Markdown and money input are not in the design and were drawn from the tokens.
  - Design decisions: an `admin:mount-islands` event replaces an entry export (Vite drops entry exports); the combobox has two stated modes; `adminFetch`'s status rules (a 413/500/504 never reports a lost session); the audit screen keeps its prev/next markup (only its URLs moved to `admin_query_path()`); `palette-trigger.js`'s dynamic `import()` is the one exception in `.claude/rules/frontend.md`.
  - Implementation: `_button` builds its attributes from a hash (a `{% set %}` capture tripped `TemplatesHaveNoRawTextTest`); an extra `StyleguideSortableIsland`, because a `Ui*` component reaches a page only through an island (1.7 KB); the `IslandExtension` tests sit in `tests/Unit/Admin/IslandManifestTest.php` (shared fixture manifest); `_table`'s planned `selectable` is `select_label` on the table and `select_name`/`select_value`/`select_label` on the row (F-m7); the browser walk used headless Chromium with #22's dev-login header instead of Claude in Chrome (accepted: the header is inert outside dev and skips only password and TOTP).
  - Fixed by the finisher from the review: Esc in the palette now resets the query and aborts a pending fetch (F-m1); `{"text": null}` answers 422 (F-m2); `_table_row` takes `actions_column: true` so a row without actions keeps its cell (F-m3, also `UiTableRow`'s `actionsColumn`); `CLAUDE.md` no longer describes `_field.html.twig` (F-m5).
- **CSP (Case A, with #16's real header):** passed by the finisher on the rebased branch (lane C, dev). `/admin/_styleguide` sends #16's policy with a per-request nonce, and `<script src="/admin-build/…/main-*.js">` carries the same nonce; the kit's `_scripts.html.twig` files load from `'self'`. The headless walk against that real header (no injecting proxy, so not the walk script's `csp` mode, which overwrites the header) passed 41 of 41 with zero `securitypolicyviolation` events, on `/admin` and the styleguide: palette (click, Ctrl+K, first use), modal, drawer, combobox, sortable list, locale tabs, Markdown preview, drop zone, dropdown. In dev the web profiler adds its own nonces and `'unsafe-inline'` to the header, which browsers ignore once a nonce is present.
- **Carried forward:**
  - **#24:** `_record_header` has no thumbnail slot (cms-venues-plan Step 8.5): use `meta` or append a `media` block. `_locale_tabs` always opens the first locale, so a 422 in another locale's panel starts hidden; add an active-panel parameter. Each `_table_row` in a table with `actions_label` passes `actions_column: true`. The walker still fills `{number}` with a UUID (`ChecksAdminRoutes.php`); teaching it is #24's Step 5.3. `unsaved-cards.js` goes into `_scripts.html.twig`, or `AdminAssetBudgetTest` fails. A combobox inside a `<dialog>`: Esc also closes the dialog (`useListbox` does not `preventDefault()`). #24's overview row omits #21, but its optional address needs #21's migration. #24 supplies `admin_api_cms_towns` (GET, `{"options": […]}`).
  - **#25:** `adminFetch` and its errors, `UiDropzone`, `UiSortableList`, `UiModal`, `UiDrawer`, `useToast`, `_sortable`, `_dropzone`, `_progress`; a `blob:` thumbnail needs a CSP change.
  - **Weak tests (F-m6, behaviour correct):** `UiSortableList.test.js`'s focus test passes without the refocus code (move `up-b` instead); `MarkdownRendererTest`'s hostile-input case does not prove the nesting limits (assert ≤ 21 `<blockquote>`); `AdminUrlExtensionTest` pushes one request, so it cannot tell `getMainRequest()` from `getCurrentRequest()`.
  - Lower-confidence notes in the review: a failed lazy palette chunk leaves the palette dead until reload; `UiSortableList` lacks `dataTransfer.setData` (Firefox drag); `assets/js/admin/*.js` is outside ESLint.
  - **Pending human:** the styleguide against `docs/reference/admin/` at 1440 and 390 px and the keyboard walk of combobox, modal, sortable list and palette in real Chrome; VoiceOver on the palette and combobox.

### 24. `cms-venues`

W6. Depends on #8 (preview and links), #9 (Venues write side), #21 (the optional address), #31 (the S14 fields), #23. Runs in parallel with #25.

- **Screens:** the venue list (CMS-1) and the venue editor (CMS-2) at `/admin/locations/{number}`, built from #23's components. The editor has one form per section (Stammdaten, Texte, Adresse & Lage, Merkmale, Hochzeit, Kontakt, Häufige Fragen, Bewertung), each saving on its own (POST → redirect → GET), plus `RecordTab` and `RecordPanel` slots. #25 fills the Fotos, Räume, Dokumente and Bewertungen tabs, and #27 the CRM panel.
- **Write side:** fine-grained `Venues` commands that implement `AuditableCommand`, e.g. `CreateVenue`, `RenameVenue` (slug + history, CMS-4), `ChangeVenueTexts`, `RelocateVenue`, `ChangeVenueTerms`, `ChangeVenueWeddingFacts`, `ChangeVenueContact`, `AnswerVenueFaq`, `RateVenue`, `ChangeVenueTier`, `PublishVenue`/`UnpublishVenue` (publishing rules of CMS-3), `DeleteVenue`. Reuse #9's handler logic; do not duplicate it. Every handler sets `venues.cms_edited_at` (new column, this cycle's migration) when the actor is a staff user, and purges (CMS-10).
- **Import guard** (CMS-11, S8): #9's import skips and reports venues with `cms_edited_at` > their last import, unless `--overwrite-cms-edits`. This changes #9's handler, which is done by then.
- **Preview** (CMS-9): a `Cms` controller at `/admin/locations/{number}/vorschau` renders #8's detail template from #8's `VenueDetailQuery` with an "include unpublished" option, plus a banner. The public detail route stays untouched, because the admin session is invisible outside `^/admin`.
- **Global search:** a `SearchProvider` for venues (name, number, town).
- **Dashboard widgets:** "Unvollständige Locations" and "Zuletzt bearbeitet".
- **Done when:**
  - application tests cover the list filters, each editor section's success and error paths (one per #4 CHECK, in the form layer), stale-edit rejection, create → publish (with and without the publishing preconditions), tier change, slug change with a working 301, delete rules per role, the preview of an unpublished venue (staff only; the public URL still answers 410/404) and the purge calls;
  - an integration test proves the import guard;
  - the screenshot comparison with the design reference passes;
  - keyboard walkthrough of the editor.

**Outcome (2026-10-30).** Done in commit `cms-venues` (review: Complete, 0 critical, 1 major, 5 minor; the major was the rebase onto #15, fixed by the finisher). Migration `Version20261030212027`, no new Composer or npm package. Staff can now list, create, edit, publish, unpublish, delete and preview venues under `/admin/locations`; the import no longer overwrites their edits. Read `CLAUDE.md` `### Venues` and `### Cms` ("Built in #24") before touching venue writes or the editor.

- **Schema:** `venues.cms_edited_at` (set by every CMS write, cleared when an import applies) and `venues.coordinates_from_town` ("Ortsmitte übernehmen"; `chk_venues_coordinates_from_town` needs coordinates). With the flag set, Seo drops the coordinates (no `og:type place`, no `geo`) and #15's `VenueMapExtension::location()` shows no map. Also `content_imports.overwrote_cms_edits` / `venues_cms_edited` and the table `venue_deleted_content_keys`, the tombstone that keeps a deleted content venue deleted.
- **Write side (`Venues`):** 12 audited commands, one handler each: `ChangeVenueBasics`, `ChangeVenueTexts`, `RelocateVenue`, `ChangeVenueTerms`, `ChangeVenueWeddingFacts`, `ChangeVenueContact`, `AnswerVenueFaq`, `RateVenue`, `PublishVenue`, `UnpublishVenue`, `DeleteVenue`, and `RecordVenueCreated` (dispatched inside `CreateVenue`, which is not audited itself). Every handler goes through `Venues\VenueEditing`: `lock()` (row lock), `open()` (lock plus the stale check), `close($venue, purgeListings)` (sets `cms_edited_at`, purges after the commit). Errors: `StaleEdit::forVenue()` (409 on the card), `VenueNotFound`, and `VenueRuleViolation` with `$field` and `$missing` (the publish gaps `summary`, `main_building`, `photo`). Slug changes keep #9's history, so the old URL answers 301.
- **Import guard (S8, CMS-11):** the import and `--prune` lock the row and skip a venue with `cms_edited_at` set. The skip is reported as `cms_edited` and counted in `content_imports`. `--force` never overwrites a CMS edit; `app:venues:import --overwrite-cms-edits` does. A venue deleted in the CMS stays deleted on the next import.
- **Screens (`Cms`):** 16 `admin_locations…` routes, all `ROLE_EDITOR`, delete also `ROLE_ADMIN` and only while unpublished. They are: the list (status tabs, search, tier, canton, without address, sort, 50 per page), `/neu`, the editor with eight cards (Stammdaten, Texte, Adresse & Lage, Merkmale, Hochzeit, Kontakt, Häufige Fragen, Bewertung; each card saves on its own, and an idle save writes nothing), publish, unpublish, delete, the preview (`/vorschau`, `noindex`, banner, no inquiry form) and the town lookup `admin_api_cms_towns`. `{number}` uses `Cms\ValueObject\VenueNumber::REQUIREMENT`. All record tabs share `_layout.html.twig` (block `record_main`), and its header comes from `VenueHeaderQuery::find()`. The sidebar item "Locations" has priority 300, above #26's 200 and 100. There is a global search provider, plus two dashboard widgets: "Unvollständige Locations" and "Zuletzt bearbeitet".
- **Shared and Admin:** `Shared\Validator\CleanText` refuses NUL bytes and invalid UTF-8. `AdminTextConstraintExtension` puts it on every back-office text field. The route walker fills `{number}` with `1` and asserts `< 500`. `_record_header` gained a `media` block and `_locale_tabs` an `active` locale. `dialog.js` now returns focus to a popover's trigger when a dialog that the popover opened closes.
- **For #25 (`cms-venue-media`), plan Step 0.3:** every name it checks landed as planned:
  - `VenueEditing::lock()` / `close(…, purgeListings)`, `StaleEdit::forVenue()`, `VenueRuleViolation` (`unwrap()`, `translationKey()`, `all()`), `VenueNotFound::forNumber()`;
  - `VenueNumber::REQUIREMENT`, `Shared\Validator\CleanText`, `Cms\Form\DecimalTextType`;
  - `_layout.html.twig` (`record_main`, param `venue`), `VenueHeaderQuery::find()`, `VenueOverviewRecordTab` (priority 1000), `_locale_tabs` `active`, `_record_header` `media`;
  - `RemoveVenueFiles::$wholeVenue`, `CmsTranslationKeysTest`, the `cms` key `error.stale`, the folder `assets/styles/cms/`.

  One item differs: there is no admin textarea CRLF extension, because Symfony 7.4's `TextareaType` already turns CRLF into LF, on every form.
- **Deviations:**
  - `CleanText` is its own validator, not a `Compound`: ICU throws on invalid UTF-8 in a message parameter. For the same reason the card `Regex`/`Email` rules use `normalizer: 'mb_scrub'`.
  - `DeleteVenue` carries `?contentKey` for its audit summary.
  - A no-op publish or unpublish is not dispatched.
  - Kontakt and Häufige Fragen are two cards; Hochzeit and Bewertung are extra cards. There is a canton select and region fields, the main building and main setting are selects, and there is no CSV button.
  - Two small fixes in #23's area: `.table-wrap` got `position: relative` (390 px overflow), and `dialog.js` got the focus return.
  - Finisher fixes from the review: the TriState questions no longer show `*`, the preview banner wording holds for published venues too, and an invalid list filter shows a notice.
  - The browser walk ran partly in headless Chromium, because Chrome's window would not resize.
- **Carry-forwards:**
  - **Test-suite memory:** the PHPUnit peak is about 215 MB of 256 MB. `AdminSession` (#22) disables the kernel reboot, so every admin GET keeps memory, the editor about 6.6 MB each. Find the retained object before adding many-request admin tests.
  - **Import race:** harden the race between a CMS delete and an import by re-checking the row after the lock in `ImportVenueContentHandler`.
  - **For a human:** VoiceOver on the town combobox and the editor's errors, and a keyboard walk in real Chrome at 390 px.

### 25. `cms-venue-media`

W6. Depends on #9 (photo pipeline) and #23. Runs in parallel with #24 through the `RecordTab` extension point; its tests create venues with factories and open its own routes (`/admin/locations/{number}/fotos`, `/raeume`, `/dokumente`, `/bewertungen`).

- **Photos** (CMS-6): the photo-manager island: a drop zone with sequential uploads and progress, a grid in position order with the first photo marked, drag-and-drop reorder plus keyboard buttons, alt text per locale, room assignment and delete, all through `/admin/api/cms/venues/{number}/photos…`. Commands `AddVenuePhoto` (stage → generate → `VenuePhoto` → `copyVariants()`, as #9), `ReorderVenuePhotos`, `DescribeVenuePhoto`, `MoveVenuePhotoToRoom`, `RemoveVenuePhoto` (deletes its files). Errors from Media (`UnreadableImage`, a too-large file) become 422s with a German message.
- **Rooms** (CMS-5): the room-editor island (list + edit drawer, reorder, delete) with `AddRoom`, `ChangeRoom`, `ReorderRooms`, `RemoveRoom`. Each recomputes the venue's room facts.
- **Documents** (CMS-7): a server page (upload form, title per locale, reorder, delete) with `AddVenueDocument` (`publishDocument()`), `RetitleVenueDocument`, `ReorderVenueDocuments`, `RemoveVenueDocument`.
- **Reviews and rating highlights** (CMS-8): a server page with `AddVenueReview`, `ChangeVenueReview`, `RemoveVenueReview`.
- Every command is auditable, sets `cms_edited_at` (the column #24 adds; whichever of #24/#25 lands second rebases onto it, so #25 writes it through the `Venue` method #24 adds, or adds that method itself if it lands first, recorded in the Outcome) and purges.
- **Done when:**
  - integration tests cover each command (files created and removed, positions contiguous, room facts recomputed, a replaced photo gets a new id);
  - application tests cover the JSON endpoints (success, 422, CSRF, role) and the server pages;
  - Vitest covers upload queueing, reorder by keyboard and error display;
  - a manual browser check uploads five photos, reorders them, and sees the new first photo on the listing card after the purge (recorded in the review);
  - ADM-8 island budget.

**Outcome (2026-10-31).** Done in commit `cms-venue-media`, after two rework rounds. The first review found 1 critical and 12 major, the re-review 2 critical and 2 major. No migration, no package. The owner cut round 2 short: "no large image uploads", "keep things simple". The lead finished it and ran the checks itself, so the last changes had no independent re-review.

- **What exists:** four record tabs on a venue, Fotos, Räume, Dokumente and Bewertungen. Fotos and Räume are the islands `PhotoManager` and `RoomEditor` on JSON endpoints under `/admin/api/cms/venues/{number}`; Dokumente and Bewertungen are server pages. Details are in `CLAUDE.md`.
- **Upload limits:** one `MEDIA_MAX_PIXELS` of 20 MP for JPEG, PNG and WebP, photos up to 10 MB, portrait up to 2:1, each refusal a 422 naming the limit. These replace 40/36/20 MP at 25 MB, where a run of uploads ended in a memory fatal, because a php-fpm worker keeps freed memory. Measured at 256M, 8 files at the limits then one of any type, in every order at 2 and 1 workers: 324 uploads, all 201, no fatal, peak 81.8 %. There is no memory guard and no 503. The import and `app:media:regenerate` keep their own limit.
- **Rework outcomes:** `stage()` renders before it stores the original, so a failed render leaves none. A reorder during an upload waits for that upload's answer instead of failing, and `resume()` was dropped for an honest banner. `planPhotos()` in the import handler gives a photo in a room without a content key its own identity (a deviation). The client refuses a file above the limit before sending it. The room drawer stays open and inert until its save answers, for 200, 422 and 409. Toasts sit at the top at every width, because at the bottom they took the click on the photo panel's Speichern.
- **Deviations:** no-op saves dispatch nothing, the move and remove forms check CSRF in the controller, and the tabs have no count pills, because `RecordTab` has no count.
- **For #19:** `client_max_body_size 26m` stays at `server` level, `php.ini` keeps `upload_max_filesize` 25M, `post_max_size` 26M and `max_execution_time` 60 above the app's limits, and D19's `purgeListings: false` invalidates the whole store anyway. **For #20:** set `MEDIA_MAX_PIXELS` and `MEDIA_MAX_PORTRAIT_RATIO` in production; a limit is raised only with `memory_limit`.
- **Owner's manual checks:** skipped by the owner, 2026-10-31 (drag in Firefox and Safari, 390 px on a real phone, an iPhone HEIC upload).

### 26. `cms-pages`

W7. Depends on #17 (landing pages) and #23. Runs in parallel with #19.

- **Landing pages** (CMS-12): a list of #17's landing pages (canton, town, building type, with venue counts and whether they pass #17's threshold) and an editor for the intro text, SEO title and meta description per locale, with live length counters (#16's ≤ 60 / ≤ 155) and a search-result preview. Stored in #17's `landing_pages` (new columns) or in `content_blocks` keyed by landing page, whichever #17 left; the public page falls back to #17's catalogue template when nothing is set.
- **Text blocks:** `content_blocks` (`key`, `locale`, `body_markdown`, `updated_at`, unique `(key, locale)`). A module declares a block (key, label, where it appears, max length, default from a catalogue) through a `ContentBlockDefinition` extension, and renders it with `{{ content_block('key') }}`, Markdown rendered safely (`league/commonmark` with HTML disabled and safe links; research confirms). Initial blocks: the listing intro under the h1 (#7) and a footer note. Saving purges the pages that show the block.
- **Done when:**
  - application tests cover the editors, the fallback, safe Markdown (a `<script>` and a `javascript:` link are neutralised), the purge, and that public pages read blocks without starting a session;
  - the screenshot comparison passes.

**Outcome (2026-10-31).** Done in commit `cms-pages` (review: Complete after one rework round; first pass 0 critical, 9 major, 10 minor; re-review 0 critical, 0 major). Migration `Version20261031070024`, no new Composer or npm package. Staff can now write a landing page's intro, SEO title and meta description, the listing intro and a footer note in the back office; without a staff text every page is #17's, byte for byte. Read `CLAUDE.md` `### Shared`, `### Search`, `### Seo` and `### Cms` ("Built in #26") before touching blocks or their pages.

- **Table:** `content_blocks` (PK `(key, locale)`, a natural key like `cantons`; `body_markdown` never blank, so clearing a field deletes the row; `updated_at` `TIMESTAMP(0)`). Keys go by identity, never by slug: `listing.intro`, `footer.note`, `landing.<canton|town|building>.<code|towns.id>.<intro|title|description>`, so a renamed slug keeps its texts. A migration that renames a building code must also update these keys.
- **Reader (`Shared\ReadModel\ContentBlocks`):** the whole table as one item `snapshot` in `cache.content_blocks` (`cache.app`, `default_lifetime: 0`). `text()`/`html()` fill a cold pool and rebuild a snapshot a day old (the soft age, measured with the clock); `peek()`/`peekHtml()` read the pool only and never query, for the footer and the error pages. `rebuild()` takes the transaction-level advisory lock inside itself, before its SELECT: `refresh()` waits for it, a cold read only tries it and, when it is taken, serves what it read and stores nothing. So a public GET never queues, and whoever saves last has read every commit before it. Any other item shape is a miss, and a cache error never fails a page.
- **Twig:** `content_block(key, fallback = null)`, `content_block_peek(key)`, `content_text(key)` (no template uses it yet). Declaring a block: `Shared\Extension\ContentBlockDefinition`; the first two are `Search\Extension\ListingIntroBlock` (600) and `Shared\Extension\FooterNoteBlock` (300). A Definition's `fallback()` renders when the caller passes none.
- **Public side:** intro under the h1 (a landing page, or the unfiltered listing on page 1), footer note after #18's links, staff title and description through `ListingMeta`, inside #16's limits; page ≥ 2 keeps #17's numbered chain. `Seo\Extension\LandingPageCatalogue` feeds the back office through `Shared\Extension\LandingPageSource`, so Cms names neither Search nor Seo.
- **Screens (`Cms`):** `admin_landing_pages` `/admin/landingpages` (type tabs, name search, 50 per page, count, threshold state, last change) and `admin_text_blocks` `/admin/textbausteine`, each with an `_edit` route, all `ROLE_EDITOR`; nav priorities 200 and 100, below Locations (300). Commands `SaveLandingTexts`, `SaveTextBlock` (audited, names only) through `Cms\ContentBlockWriter`, then `RefreshContentBlockSnapshot`, then `PurgeListingsCache`. Live counters, Markdown preview, and the search-result preview `assets/js/admin/serp-preview.js` (400 B gzip); a stale save is a 409 with the input kept.
- **Rework:**
  - A failed refresh logs, the save stays a 303 and the purge still runs. The save stands; saving the same text again changes nothing, so the snapshot's age heals it.
  - The listing preloads the 600 weight only when the intro holds `<strong` or a heading (`<hr />` also triggers it, harmless, because the page's h1 needs that face anyway).
  - `render()` maps `<h1>` to `<h2>`, so a page keeps one h1. A body with no visible text counts as no block.
  - The cold query pin moved from 8 to 11 (begin, try-lock, SELECT, commit), then 7; the warm pins stay 7/7/7/8.
  - Lighthouse mobile on `/hochzeitslocations/zuerich`, Performance / CLS: no row 100 / 0.024; plain paragraph 99 / 0.024; three bold runs 97 / 0 (was 83 / 0.338); bold, link, `##` and list 100 / 0 (was 93 / 0.154). The re-review ran these while phpunit ran in the same lane, so Performance may read low; CLS was not repeated on an idle lane. `/merkliste` with a bold note stays in budget at 0.0439.
  - Staff text cannot scroll the page sideways, and a list's marker stays beside its text (mixin `abstracts/_staff-text.scss`).
- **Deviations:**
  - The version token hashes `key|locale|updated_at as seconds|sha256(body)`, not `key@updated_at`, because Doctrine writes whole seconds and a same-second revision must still be a 409.
  - The writer refreshes before it purges, so a page rendered after the purge reads the new text, and skips both when nothing changed.
  - The writer's lock uses `hashtextextended`, #14's 64-bit form. Bodies are stored trimmed.
  - A custom landing title also applies on the noindex `?canton=zh` form; it stays noindex and canonicalises to the clean path.
  - A footer-note save purges listings only, so detail and legal pages lag up to their `s-maxage`. This meets `### 26`'s "saving purges" only in part, and that is the decided state until #19.
  - #17's intro moved from `<p class="listing-hero__intro">` to `<div …><p>`, text unchanged.
  - **`var/share/<env>/pools/app`:** `cache.app` lives there, so `cache:clear` does not empty this pool. A direct SQL change needs `make sf c="cache:pool:clear cache.content_blocks"`; otherwise it heals within a day, and in about two days once #19 lands (read from #19's TTLs, not run), because listings then stay cached until Zurich midnight and `text()` runs only on a PHP render.
- **Accepted limit:** a connection lost before the bus's COMMIT of the refresh message can still answer 500. The row is committed, and the purge runs after a reconnect, the same exposure as every post-commit purge since #9.
- **Tests:** `make test` after the rebase: PHPUnit OK, 3075 tests, 36794 assertions, peak 223 MB (under the 240 MB gate, so no `memory_limit` change); Vitest 15 files, 150 tests. Assertion counts differed between earlier runs with the same 3073 tests (36793, 36631, 36769), cause not found; the tests are identical in number, and the finisher's run is the clean gate.
- **Carry-forward:**
  - #23's `char-counter.js` counts a line break as 2 and the server counts 1. It overstates and never under-reports, so it stays a kit fix.
  - #19: `content_blocks` under its long TTLs (the two-day figure above; reword `CLAUDE.md` when it lands), and a footer-note save then empties the whole store, which ends the detail-page lag.
  - #20: `var/share` decides whether the pool survives a deploy. A cold pool caches a peek-only page without the note, so keep `var/share/prod/pools` or hit a listing before the first clear.
  - Owner's manual checks: Google rich-result test of a canton page with a custom title and description; Firefox and Safari for `serp-preview.js`; screenshot sign-off (no artboard exists for these screens, so Implement and Review judged them against the nearest patterns; the images stayed in the removed worktree, so look at `/admin/landingpages` and `/admin/textbausteine` directly).

## Back office: CRM (W9–W10, after launch)

The CRM goes live after #20 (Q8); #27 reached main earlier, on the owner's go. Read S12 and S13 first. Its cycles deploy through #20's workflow, and every migration must be safe on production data (additive, no table rewrites).

### 27. `crm-foundation`

W9. Depends on #24 (the venue editor's panel slot and the venue `SearchProvider` pattern). Split into #27a `crm-foundation` and #27b `crm-timeline`, one commit each; #28–#30 depend on #27b. Its production steps wait for #20: see the Outcome.

- **Schema** (S13): `crm_companies`, `crm_contacts`, `crm_company_venues` (`company_id`, `venue_id` unique, so a venue belongs to at most one company), `crm_activity_types` (seeded rows), `crm_activities`, `crm_activity_companies`, `crm_activity_contacts`, `crm_tasks`. Vocabularies per S13. Entities in `Crm\Entity`, read models in `Crm\ReadModel`.
- **Screens:** company and contact lists (CRM-6), record pages (CRM-3) with inline property editing (one small JSON write per field, through commands), the timeline composer island (CRM-4), and tasks (CRM-5) with the task list, record task panels and dashboard widgets ("Meine Aufgaben", "Überfällig"). The timeline renderer registry `admin-ui/src/timeline/types.js` (hotspot, one line per type) and the `TimelineActivityType` extension point. The audit-log events of a company's venues are shown read-only.
- **Bootstrap** (CRM-1): `app:crm:bootstrap` + the button on the company list.
- **Venue panel:** a `RecordPanel` on the CMS venue editor shows the company, its contacts and its open tasks, and offers "Firma anlegen" when there is none.
- **Global search:** `SearchProvider`s for companies and contacts.
- **Privacy** (CRM-11): contact export and erasure (`admin`).
- **Done when:**
  - application tests cover the lists (search, filters, sort, CSV export per role), the record pages, inline edits (success, 422, stale edit), timeline entries (each type; edit and delete only your own), tasks, bootstrap idempotence, the venue panel, export and erasure;
  - Vitest covers the composer and the inline editor;
  - the screenshot comparison with the design reference passes;
  - on production, `app:crm:bootstrap` runs once and its counts are recorded in the Outcome.

**Outcome (2026-10-31).** Covers #27a and #27b together: commits `crm-foundation-a` (#27a) and `crm-timeline` (#27b). Reviews: #27a Complete, #27b Complete with Minors. No new Composer or npm package, no Makefile target, no cron. Working notes for later cycles: `.claude/rules/module-crm.md`, `.claude/rules/module-admin.md`.

- **Schema:** one migration, `Version20261031102722` (additive, with `down()`, alters no existing table; #27b adds none), mapping `orm.mappings.Crm`. Tables `crm_companies`, `crm_contacts`, `crm_company_venues`, `crm_activity_types`, `crm_activities`, `crm_activity_companies`, `crm_activity_contacts`, `crm_tasks`; one entity each in `App\Crm\Entity`. `crm_company_venues.venue_id` is unique and cascades with the venue, so `DeleteVenue` is unchanged and the company stays. `crm_contacts.email` is lower-case with a plain unique key (`uk_crm_contacts_email`). A task needs `due_on` and has at most one object (`chk_crm_tasks_one_object`), none when created from the task list. Seeded types: `note`, `email_logged`, `call`, `meeting`. The unmapped GIN indexes `idx_crm_{companies,contacts}_search_key` serve the search.
- **Pages (#27a):** `admin_crm_companies` `/admin/crm/firmen` and `admin_crm_contacts` `/admin/crm/kontakte`: search, filters, sort in the query string, 50 per page, with `…_new` (`/neu`), `…_show` (`/{id}`) and `…_export` (`/export`, CSV, admin only). Company writes: `admin_crm_companies_link_venue`, `…_unlink_venue`, `…_delete` (admin only), `…_bootstrap`. Contact privacy: `admin_crm_contacts_data_export` (`/{id}/export`, JSON) and `admin_crm_contacts_erase` (`/{id}/loeschen`), both admin only. Record pages share `templates/admin/crm/_record_layout.html.twig`.
- **Pages (#27b):** `admin_crm_tasks` `/admin/crm/aufgaben?view=meine|heute|ueberfaellig|alle` with a create form below the table, `admin_crm_tasks_toggle` (POST `/{id}/status`, plain form, the form states the wanted state), the Aufgaben card on both records, the timeline in the middle column of both records.
- **JSON (`/admin/api/crm/…`):** `admin_api_crm_company_property`, `admin_api_crm_contact_property` (PATCH), lookups `admin_api_crm_companies`, `admin_api_crm_venues`, `admin_api_crm_activity_log|edit|delete`, `admin_api_crm_{company,contact}_timeline`, `admin_api_crm_tasks`. `App\Crm\CrmApi` parses payloads and maps failures: 422 per field, 409 `{"errors": {"": […]}}` for a stale value, 403 and 404 as JSON. An unchanged inline value answers 200 without a dispatch, so it leaves no audit row (`AuditLogMiddleware` has no skip).
- **Writes:** `App\Crm\Command\{CreateCompany, DeleteCompany, CreateCompanyForVenue, LinkVenue, UnlinkVenue, CreateContact, EraseContact, ChangeCompanyProperty, ChangeContactProperty, LogActivity, EditActivity, DeleteActivity, CreateTask, CompleteTask, ReopenTask}`, all audited; a summary never holds a value or a text. Every business error is in `App\Crm\Exception`.
- **Timeline:** `App\Crm\ReadModel\TimelineQuery::page()` merges `crm_activities` with the audit rows of a company's venues (`TimelineQuery::SYSTEM_COMMANDS`, read through `Admin\ReadModel\AuditLogQuery::forSubjects()`), newest first, keyset on `(time, id)`, 20 per page. System rows show only on a company without a type filter. `App\Crm\TimelineProps::of()` builds the island's props with the first page, so opening a record makes no request. Island `timeline` (`TimelineIsland.vue`, `crm/TimelineComposer.vue`, `crm/TimelineList.vue`, `crm/entries.js`), renderer map `admin-ui/src/timeline/types.js`, quick actions `_quick_actions.html.twig`. Edit and delete are the author's only, also for an admin, and never on an anonymised entry or a type staff cannot write (`App\Crm\OwnActivity::lock()`, `TimelineEntry::canBeEditedBy()`). An entry on a contact also links the contact's company.
- **Extension points:** new `Admin\Extension\TimelineActivityType` (`code()`, `label()`, `priority()`, `requiredRole()`, `composable()`) and `TimelineActivityTypeRegistry`. `App\Crm\Extension`: `{Note, LoggedEmail, Call, Meeting}ActivityType` (400, 300, 200, 100), `CompaniesNavigationItem` 500, `ContactsNavigationItem` 400, `TasksNavigationItem` 100 (with the badge), `MyTasksWidget` 400, `OverdueTasksWidget` 300, `VenueCrmPanel` 100, `CompanySearchProvider` 200, `ContactSearchProvider` 100. Free for later cycles: Crm nav 300 (#28) and 200 (#29), widget 500 (#29).
- **Tasks:** `App\Crm\ReadModel\TaskListQuery` (`page()`, `openOf()`, `mine()`, `overdue()`), `TaskBadge` (the viewer's open tasks due today or earlier; a column of `OwnerOptionsQuery::all()` where that runs, else one statement), `App\Crm\Form\ReturnPath::safe()` for `return_to`. "Today" is Europe/Zurich, `TaskDueDate::today()`.
- **Bootstrap:** `app:crm:bootstrap` (`App\Crm\Console\BootstrapCommand`) and the button share `App\Crm\CompanyBootstrap::run()`. One transaction per venue, failures reported as `<number>: <reason>` with no contact value, exit 1. The button takes `BootstrapController::BATCH` (100) per POST. Expect few contacts on production: no import writes `contact_person`, and the report says so.
- **Venue panel:** `VenueCrmPanel` on every venue tab: company, contacts, open tasks read-only, or "Firma anlegen" (`admin_crm_company_from_venue`). One statement (`VenueCompanyQuery::forVenue()`).
- **Privacy:** `App\Crm\ContactEraser::erase()` deletes the contact, anonymises shared entries, deletes the others, and blanks a matching address on the company; the copy on a venue stays and the dialog says so. `ContactExportQuery` gives the JSON. New Datenschutz section `legal.privacy.crm` ("Kontakte von Locations", `shared` catalogue) with a `# TODO owner`.
- **CSV:** `App\Crm\CsvStream`: `;`, UTF-8 BOM, CRLF, `fputcsv` with `escape: ''`; a cell starting with a formula character gets a leading `'`, so E.164 phones carry it too (P13). A failure before the first row is a real error page; later the file ends in a notice line, and the log names class, file and line only.
- **Twig and strings:** functions `crm_tone()`, `crm_tones()`, `crm_property_labels()`, `crm_company_fields()`, `crm_contact_fields()`, `crm_task_due()`. Catalogue domain `crm`, plus `crm.*` in `validators` and `legal.privacy.crm.*` in `shared`.
- **How a later cycle adds an entry type:** a row in its own migration, a `TimelineActivityType`, a line in `types.js` (`TimelineTypesPinTest` fails without it), `activity.type.<code>` and `activity.tab.<code>` for every type, `activity.compose.<code>.{placeholder,submit}` for a composable one. `RecordKind::Deal` makes PHPStan flag every `match` that needs it.
- **Figures:** on the final tree before the rebase, `make lint` and `make perf-check` green, `make test` PHPUnit 3,961 tests, Vitest 346. At B7: Timeline island 8,756 B gzip, shared JS 35,192 B, admin CSS 12,988 B. Query pins in production terms: company list 5 (ceiling 5), contact list 4, task list 4, company record 7, contact record 5, dashboard 7 (ceiling 7).
- **Deviations from the design and the text:** added Rechtlicher Name, Website and Notizen as properties, a Meeting composer tab, due date and assignee in the Aufgabe tab; "E-Mail" logs manually until #30. No "Aufgaben" timeline filter (tasks live in the right card). No design exists for the company list, contact page, contact list, task list or "Firma anlegen"; they follow `Locations` and `Company`. S13's "Heute fällig" is a pill in Meine Aufgaben, and Überfällig is the cycle's own widget. Task toggles are submit buttons, not checkboxes, so they work without JS. Without JS the properties and the timeline are read-only.
- **Deviations from the plan:** a company's card and the venue panel also collect the tasks on its contacts. The venue panel's tasks have no toggles. `TasksNavigationItem` injects `TaskBadge`, not `Security`, and the count rides on the owners statement. `forSubjects()` cuts on `changes->'changed'` in SQL, so tier-less edits cannot starve a page. `ReturnPath::safe()` refuses `/admin`, so a dashboard toggle sends `/admin/`.
- **Decisions on the review's findings:** B-M1 (a double press created a task twice) was downgraded to a Minor by the owner and fixed: `saving` stays set after a created task, proved by a Vitest test, with no double-press trial. B-m1 stays: plain forms double-POST on a double click across the whole back office, "Aufgabe erstellen" included. Heute and Überfällig show everyone's tasks, and a row names its assignee. The Aufgaben badge stays pink, because `.sidebar__badge` has one tone and `NavigationItem::badge(): ?int` has none, where the design draws it dark grey; #29's Anfragen badge shares that decision. The task list's wrap after `Firma ·` at 375 to 430 px stays. S11's real-browser check was waived by the owner; headless Chromium at 390 and 1440 px, the keyboard walkthrough, the validator and Lighthouse (Accessibility 100) stand in for it.
- **Carried forward:**
  - **Owed on production, after #20's Phases 7-9, owner present (plan F.4):** `pg_dump`, `app:crm:bootstrap --no-debug` once, record the counts here, and measure the button's 100 per POST against Neon; above 60 s, lower `BATCH`. Done when's last bullet stays open until then.
  - On the real host: download a CSV through `HttpCache`; a delay is acceptable, loss is not.
  - Owner: screenshot sign-off of the screens without a design and comparison of company record, dashboard and venue panel with `docs/reference/admin/`; review of `legal.privacy.crm`.
  - F-m3: a contact search `q` is always a Seq Scan, about 50 ms per statement at 10,000 rows, 2 to 4 ms at 300; the name search alone uses its index. No action at this size.
  - F-m7, #29: erase and company delete can deadlock. `app:crm:purge` through `erase()` must take the company lock before the contact, or retry. The fix sketch is untested.
  - #20: its plan still says `app:crm:*` join the daily cron in "#27/#28". They are #28's and #29's; #27 adds no cron.
  - #28: `TimelineQuery` names `App\Venues\Command\ChangeVenueTier` as a string, so #28's tier command takes that name. `ActivityRepository::deleteUnlinked()` needs `crm_activity_deals`. `stage` is read from the column everywhere, so #28 only writes it.
  - #29: the dashboard is at its ceiling of 7, and Neue Anfragen adds a statement. `ContactExportQuery` gains inquiries.
  - Small and known: the bootstrap toast hides after 6 s; 100 permanently failing venues hold back a button run, not the console; the panel's `seit {year}` is `created_at`; an erased person can come back from a venue's `contact_person` until that copy is removed; `<title>` lags after a rename; HEAD on an export runs the query.

### 28. `crm-deals`

W10. Depends on #27b. Parallel with #29 and #30.

- **Schema:** `crm_deal_stages` (seeded: Neu, Kontaktiert, Angebot, Verhandlung, Gewonnen, Verloren; `position`, `probability`, `is_won`, `is_lost`), `crm_deals` (S13 fields; `tier` from `Venues\ValueObject\Tier`), `crm_deal_contacts`, `crm_activity_deals`, the `deal_stage_changed` activity type, and `crm_tasks.deal_id`, which replaces #27's `chk_crm_tasks_one_object` with a CHECK over three objects.
- **Screens:** the pipeline board island (CRM-7, sums per column, filter by owner and close month) with a list view alternative, the deal record page, and deals on company and venue records (a `RecordTab` on the company and a line in #27's venue panel).
- **Won and renewals** (CRM-8): "Tier auf Location anwenden", the "Verlängerungen" widget, `app:crm:renewal-tasks` (cron, added to #20's crontab), "Verlängern". The company `stage` is now derived (S13).
- **Done when:**
  - application tests cover the deal CRUD, stage moves (JSON, CSRF, keyboard menu path), applying the tier (venue tier changed, audited, purged), renewal-task idempotence and the derived company stage;
  - Vitest covers the board's drag and the keyboard move;
  - the screenshot comparison passes.

**Outcome (2026-11-01).** Parts A and B landed as one commit, `crm-deals`; the plan's split into #28a and #28b was dropped by the lead. Review: Needs Rework (0/1/7), one rework round, re-review **Complete**. The owner gave the landing go on 2026-11-01 and skipped the screenshot check, the defaults (lost reasons, stage probabilities, renewal due date, all left as built) and the Firefox and Safari checks, so "the screenshot comparison passes" stands waived. #28 did not wait for #20. Gate on the re-reviewed tree, before the rebase (`var/rereview-1/gate-*.log`): PHPUnit 4,384 tests, 53,582 assertions; Vitest 391 tests in 29 files; `make perf-check` 13 tests, 207 assertions. No new Composer or npm package, no Makefile target. Working notes: `.claude/rules/module-crm.md`.

- **Schema:** one migration, `Version20261031232837` (additive, with `down()`). `crm_deal_stages` (code-keyed rows `new`, `contacted`, `offer`, `negotiation`, `won`, `lost`, probabilities 10, 25, 50, 75, 100, 0), `crm_deals`, `crm_deal_contacts`, `crm_activity_deals`; `crm_tasks` gains `deal_id` (CASCADE) and `is_renewal`, `chk_crm_tasks_one_object` now covers three objects, plus `chk_crm_tasks_renewal` and the partial unique `uk_crm_tasks_renewal`. Type `deal_stage_changed` takes position 5. `amount_rappen` is a nullable `BIGINT` with no upper limit in the database; forms and JSON stop at CHF 99'999'999.99 (`Chf::MAX_RAPPEN`), so a board's sums stay safe in JS. `company_id` and `owner_id` are RESTRICT, `venue_id` is SET NULL, because Venues may not know the CRM. `renewed_from_id` is UNIQUE, so a deal is renewed once. Entities `App\Crm\Entity\{Deal, DealStage, DealContact, ActivityDeal}`.
- **Pages:** `admin_crm_deals` `/admin/crm/deals` is the board, `?ansicht=liste` the server list, with `…_new` (`/neu`, company first, then the deal), `…_export` (CSV, admin only) and the record `…_show` (`/{id}`). Record writes, all POST with PRG: `…_stage` (`/phase`, the no-JS and keyboard path to a stage), `…_apply_tier` (`/tier`), `…_renew` (`/verlaengern`), `…_link_contact`, `…_unlink_contact`, `…_delete` (admin only). `admin_crm_companies_deals` is a company's Deals tab, served by `CompanyDealsRecordTab` (500) beside `CompanyOverviewRecordTab` (1000). A Deals card sits on the company and contact records (`App\Crm\ReadModel\DealCardQuery`, `deal/_card.html.twig`), and the venue panel shows the open deal, its stage and its value.
- **JSON:** `admin_api_crm_deal_property` (PATCH, `App\Crm\ValueObject\DealProperty` is the allow-list; a reply also carries `display` and `draft`, because a stored amount is Rappen and is neither shown nor edited as such), `admin_api_crm_deal_stage` (PATCH `{stage, expected, lostReason?}`, 409 when stale), `admin_api_crm_deal_timeline` (GET). The board is the island `deal-board`: native drag, "Weiter →" and "Verschieben nach" for the keyboard, optimistic moves with rollback, at most 100 open and the 20 newest closed cards per column, with head totals from the server (`App\Crm\ReadModel\DealBoardQuery`).
- **Writes:** `App\Crm\Command\{CreateDeal, ChangeDealProperty, MoveDealStage, ChangeDealLostReason, DeleteDeal, LinkDealContact, UnlinkDealContact, ApplyDealTier, RenewDeal, CreateRenewalTask}`, all audited, and `RefreshCompanyStages`, not audited. `App\Venues\Command\ChangeVenueTier` is the Venues side of "Tier auf Location anwenden": `ApplyDealTier` dispatches it unstamped, so both commit together and the purge runs after the commit. It sets `cms_edited_at` on purpose, so that venue stops taking folder updates (S8). Lock order everywhere: company, deal, venue.
- **Derived stage:** `App\Crm\CompanyStageSync` is the one writer (S13). `RecordKind::Deal` extends #27's timeline and tasks; a stage change goes on the deal's and the company's timeline, never a contact's. A company with a deal cannot be deleted (`CompanyHasDeals`).
- **Renewals:** `App\Crm\Extension\RenewalsWidget` (250), `app:crm:renewal-tasks [--dry-run]`, `RenewDeal`. The window is `App\Crm\ValueObject\RenewalWindow`: won, ends today through 60 days on, not yet renewed. The task is due 30 days before the end or today when that has passed, and goes to the deal's owner, else the company's, else the oldest active admin. `App\Crm\ValueObject\ContractPeriod` keeps whole years and months over a leap year. Deal tasks also show on the company card, the venue panel and the task rows.
- **Cron:** this commit held no cron line, because `bin/cron-daily` is #20's. #20's commit `prod-deploy` added the two: after the purge line, `step crm-refresh-stages "$PHP" bin/console --no-interaction --quiet app:crm:refresh-stages` and the same for `crm-renewal-tasks` with `app:crm:renewal-tasks`. Both commands are silent under `--quiet` on a good night and tested so. They run once #20's Phase 7 enters the crontab. Until then neither runs by itself: a contract's end flips no stage and no renewal task is written. Both can be run by hand. `docs/rpi-cycles.md`'s #20 Cron bullet and `prod-deploy-plan.md:278` in the tracking directory are corrected.
- **Also:** Twig filter `chf`, functions `crm_deal_fields()` and `crm_deal_tone()`; catalogue `crm` (`deal.*`, `renewal.*`, `activity.*.deal_stage_changed` and others), `validators` (`crm.deal.*`, `crm.property.{amount,date}`), `cms` (`history.command.ChangeVenueTier`). Nav Deals 300, search 50 (`App\Crm\Extension\DealSearchProvider`), timeline type 50. A contact's data export lists their deals, and an erasure removes the link and keeps the deal.
- **For later cycles:**
  - #29: `app:crm:purge` must keep couples with a deal: add `NOT EXISTS` on `crm_deal_contacts` (recorded in the cross-cycle notes). `inquiry` takes position **6**, nav 200, widget 500. The dashboard is at its ceiling of 8 in production terms, so Neue Anfragen needs a new ceiling.
  - #30: `email_sent` takes position **7**.
- **Deviations:** Parts landed as one commit. Beyond the plan: `ChangeDealLostReason`, from the review (the reason of a lost deal could not be changed, and a success flash said it was); the list's `q` also searches the company's name; a stage change stores the deal's name as `title`, because `chk_crm_activities_content` needs one; closed columns order by `closed_at`, and a moved card lands at the top of its column until a reload; the widget's head says "5 von 6, nächste 60 Tage" when capped; a stale tier apply is a flash, not a 409, because the route is PRG. Left out of the design: the KPI tiles "Offene Deals" and "Verlängerungen in 60 Tagen" (D23) and the bold stage name in the timeline sentence. Added to the design: the board's "Filtern" button (filters work without JS), the record's stage form, the stage pills' tones. `.deal-board .kanban-column` is 292 px against the kit's 268. Step A9.3 (`legal.privacy.crm.deals`) was skipped by the owner's rule.
- **Decided, left as they are (owner):** F-m3, two contacts with one name collapse into one option in the record's link form (`DealContactType`; the create form is fine). F-m4, a contact's timeline offers an always empty "Deal-Phasen" tab. F-m5, Verlängern also copies a contact who has moved to another company and a venue no longer linked.
- **Decided (owner, 2026-11-01), landed as `crm-renewal-follow`:** an open renewal task follows its deal. A deal leaving won or an emptied `contract_end` completes it; a new `contract_end` on a won deal moves its due date through `App\Crm\ValueObject\RenewalWindow::taskDueOn()`, the one place for the 30 days and the clamp to today.
- **Decided, left as it is (lead, owner told):** a done renewal task stays done. After won → negotiation → won, or an end emptied and refilled, `uk_crm_tasks_renewal` writes no new one, so the deal has no renewal task unless staff reopen it; the widget still lists it inside the 60 days.
- **Known, no action:** after a venue moves from one company to another, the new company's panel shows the old company's open deal (`VenueCompanyQuery`'s `LATERAL` has no company condition). A start on the 29th to 31st with an end at the end of February gives a period that is not whole months; the dates stay editable. `app:crm:renewal-tasks` prints the exception's message, which only the owner reads.
- **Not proved:** a half-typed date and `autofocus` in a dialog on Firefox and Safari, Enter on the closed select on macOS, and a real phone. S11 and the walkthroughs ran on headless Chromium, because Chrome stops at password and TOTP.

### 29. `crm-inquiries`

W10. Depends on #14 and #27b. Parallel with #28 and #30.

- **Ingestion** (CRM-9): a subscriber to #14's `InquirySubmitted` event creates or updates the `couple` contact and writes `crm_inquiry_links` (`inquiry_id` FK → `inquiries` ON DELETE CASCADE, `contact_id`, `activity_id`, `status`), plus the `inquiry` activity type and timeline entries on the contact and on the venue's company. A failure is logged and reported to Sentry, and never surfaces to the guest.
- **Inbox:** `/admin/crm/anfragen` with status, venue, guests, date and mail outcome; "Neue Anfragen" on the dashboard and as a sidebar badge.
- **Backfill:** `app:crm:backfill-inquiries` (idempotent), run once on production.
- **Retention** (CRM-11): `app:crm:purge` (cron) and its interplay with `app:inquiries:purge`.
- **Datenschutz:** extend #27's section `legal.privacy.crm` on #18's Datenschutz page (internal processing of inquiry data for customer care; retention) and list it in the Outcome for the owner's review.
- **Done when:**
  - integration tests cover ingestion (new contact, existing contact by case-insensitive e-mail, a venue without a company), that a failing CRM never fails the inquiry, the backfill idempotence and the retention;
  - application tests cover the inbox;
  - the backfill's counts on production are recorded in the Outcome.

**Outcome (2026-11-01).** One commit, `crm-inquiries`. Review: **Complete** on the first pass, 0/0/2, no rework. F-m1 (the dashboard table's region repeated the card's name, axe `landmark-unique` on `/admin`) was fixed at landing with `inquiry.widget.table_label`; F-m2 (a seventh filter tab pushes "Deal-Phasen" off the bar at 1440, the bar scrolls) is accepted. The owner gave the go for Implement on 2026-11-01 and the standing landing permission the same day. The Inbox and dashboard comparison with `docs/reference/admin/` at 1440 and 390 px ran on headless Chromium only; no person has looked at it, and Firefox, Safari and a real phone are not proved. **Production backfill count: open, after launch**, so that "Done when" line is not met yet: the first nightly mail carries the figure, or a one-off run of the command. Gate on the reviewed tree, before the landing fix: PHPUnit 4,654 tests; Vitest 395 tests in 29 files; `make perf-check` 13 tests; `make deploy-test` passes. No new Composer or npm package, no Makefile target. Working notes: `.claude/rules/module-crm.md`.

- **Schema:** one migration, `Version20261101115604` (additive, with `down()`, up and down proved on a copy of populated data). `crm_inquiry_links`: key `inquiry_id` → `inquiries` CASCADE, so the row goes with the inquiry under `app:inquiries:purge`; `contact_id` and `activity_id` SET NULL, so the row outlives an erased contact; `status` `new`, `seen`, `done` (`App\Crm\ValueObject\InquiryStatus`, `chk_crm_inquiry_links_status`). Type `inquiry` takes position 6. Entity `App\Crm\Entity\InquiryLink`, whose `inquiry` is `OneToOne` as Doctrine refuses a many-to-one as the sole id; the schema is the same.
- **Ingestion (CRM-9):** `App\Crm\EventListener\IngestInquiryListener` on `InquirySubmitted` dispatches `App\Crm\Command\IngestInquiry` and catches `\Throwable`, so nothing reaches the guest; it logs the class and the place, never the message. `IngestInquiryHandler` finds the `couple` contact by e-mail or creates it, and writes the entry, its timeline links (the contact and the venue's company) and the link row. A known e-mail keeps staff's values; only a blank first name or phone is filled. The entry's title is the venue's name, so no personal data is copied, and the mail outcome and the couple's name are read live from `inquiries`. Nothing under `src/Inquiries` changed.
- **Inbox:** `admin_crm_inquiries` `/admin/crm/anfragen` (`?status=new|seen|done|all`, default `new`), `admin_crm_inquiries_show` `/{id}`, `admin_crm_inquiries_status` POST `/{id}/status`, `admin_crm_inquiries_resend` POST `/{id}/senden`, all `ROLE_EDITOR`. Status tabs with counts, a list beside a detail pane (`Inbox.dc.html`), "Kontakt öffnen". `App\Crm\Command\ChangeInquiryStatus` is audited with the status code only. Reads: `App\Crm\ReadModel\{InquiryInboxQuery, InquiryInboxPage, InquiryInboxCounts, InquiryInboxRow, InquiryDetail}`. Styles: `assets/styles/crm/_inbox.scss`. A GET never changes a status.
- **Erneut senden** (not in §29): `App\Crm\Controller\InquiryResendController` dispatches `App\Inquiries\Command\DeliverInquiry` on a venue mail `failed` or `pending`, not audited. It is the cure for what `app:inquiries:report-stuck` prints, and `docs/operations.md` now says so. The unchanged handler also sends the couple's copy once the venue mail went out, so two mails can leave while the flash names only the location.
- **Dashboard and sidebar:** `App\Crm\Extension\NewInquiriesWidget` (500, the five newest of any status), `InquiriesNavigationItem` "Anfragen" (200) with `App\Crm\ReadModel\InquiryBadge`, which mirrors `TaskBadge`. The dashboard's ceiling goes from 8 to 10, 9 in test terms, as a count and rows are two statements.
- **Timeline:** `App\Crm\Extension\InquiryActivityType` (75, not composable; a staff member can neither write nor edit nor delete an entry of it). `TimelineQuery::ACTIVITY_JOINS` reads the inquiry through `LEFT JOIN`s, `TimelineEntry` gains `inquiryId` and `inquiryCouple`, the island a renderer and "Anfrage öffnen", and the filter a tab. Both go null after the purge or an anonymisation, so no dead link stays.
- **Commands:** `app:crm:backfill-inquiries` ingests every inquiry with no link row, is idempotent, and prints nothing at 0. `app:crm:purge [--dry-run]` erases what `App\Crm\ReadModel\CoupleRetentionQuery::expired()` returns: couples older than `%inquiries.retention_months%` with no recent entry and no deal, one `EraseContact` per id, with `NOT EXISTS` on `crm_deal_contacts` as #28 asked. A couple past the cut-off with an open task is erased with it. Each failure prints `<id>: <class>`, never a message, and exits 1. **Cron:** both are in `bin/cron-daily`, after `crm-renewal-tasks` and before `inquiries-stuck`, so a stuck id in the same mail already has its inbox page. `crm-purge` runs with `--quiet`; `crm-backfill` without it, as a repaired inquiry is worth the mail. `tests/Deploy/run.sh` pins seven commands. They run once #20's Phase 7 enters the crontab; until then neither runs by itself.
- **Also:** catalogue `crm` (`inquiry.*`, `nav.inquiries`, `activity.*.inquiry`, `timeline.inquiry.*`); pill tone groups `inquiry` and `mail` in `crm_tones()`; `CrmStory` writes six inquiries through `IngestInquiry`, with `failed` and `pending` rows for the resend. `make admin-build` is needed after a pull.
- **For later cycles:**
  - #30: `email_sent` takes position **7**. #29 reads no `payload` and it stays `{}`; #30 adds its own column after `inquiryCouple` in `TimelineQuery::ACTIVITY_COLUMNS`, `TimelineEntry` and `toArray()`. `CrmSchemaMigrationTest` counts 13 primary keys, and the type list ends in `inquiry`. `TimelineTypesPinTest` reads no `renderer`, so a new type needs its own Vitest case.
- **Deviations:** §29's Datenschutz bullet is dropped by the owner, so `legal.privacy.crm` is unchanged and T9 is gone. No Sentry call: a failed ingestion is logged and repaired or named by the nightly backfill. The backfill runs nightly, not once. Inquiries stay out of `ContactExportQuery`, and #27's "gains inquiries" falls. The inbox has no `q` or `mail` filter and no KPI tile, as the design has neither filter and retention bounds the list. "Alle" carries a count and list rows show a mail pill when the mail is not `sent`. Dates are absolute. The timeline shows no facts line. The rest of the deviations are under the changes log's `Additional or Deviating Changes`.
- **Decided, left as they are:** F-m7, erase against a company delete takes no lock order: each couple is one dispatch, a failed id is retried the next night, and it takes a staff delete of that couple's company at 03:15. Two first inquiries from one address at the same instant hit `uk_crm_contacts_email`; the loser is logged and the night's backfill ingests it. An inquiry from a known `venue_contact` stays off that contact's own company unless the venue belongs to it. An inquiry not yet ingested stays out of the inbox and the widget until the backfill has it.
- **Known, no action:** widget rows carry no link to the inquiry. At 390 px the widget's Status column scrolls and the "Alle" tab wraps. `app:crm:backfill-inquiries` has no `--dry-run`. `OwnerOptionsQuery::all()` skips the badge with no staff row, which no back-office page can see.

### 30. `crm-email`

W10. Depends on #27b (and #20's mail setup for the production check). Parallel with #28 and #29.

- **Schema:** `crm_email_templates` (name, subject, Markdown body), the `email_sent` activity type. A sent mail is that activity, with `{to, cc?, status}` in its payload, status `sent|failed`; there is no `crm_emails`.
- **E-Mail tab of the timeline composer** (CRM-10) on contact and company records: recipient, template picker, Cc, subject, Markdown body, send. The handler renders HTML + text like #14's mails, sends synchronously (S1: no workers), records the status, and honours `INQUIRY_RECIPIENT_OVERRIDE` outside production. Templates are managed on a settings screen (`admin` only).
- **Done when:**
  - integration tests cover rendering with merge fields (missing values render empty, no Twig injection: templates are not Twig, merge fields are a fixed list), sending through the Mailer test transport, the override and a failed send;
  - application tests cover the endpoints and the template screen;
  - on production, a test e-mail to the owner arrives and passes SPF/DKIM/DMARC (open after launch, not a gate, recorded in the Outcome).

**Outcome (2026-11-01).** One commit, `crm-email`. Review: Needs Rework on the first pass (0 Critical, 1 Major, 15 Minor), one rework round, then **Complete after Rereview 1: 0 Critical, 0 Major**, and its 2 Minors fixed at landing. The owner's standing word of 2026-11-01 ("never wait on my go") let it land on Complete with no further go. Smaller than §30, whose text above now says what was built: no `crm_emails` table and no `CRM_MAIL_OVERRIDE`. Staff send a one-to-one mail from a company or contact record, from a template with fixed merge fields, synchronously in the request, and it lands on the timeline as `email_sent` with status `sent` or `failed`. **Production check: open, after #20 and #30 are live**, so that "Done when" line is not met yet (last bullet). Gate on the re-reviewed tree, before the rebase: PHPUnit 4,787 tests; Vitest 420 tests in 29 files, 421 with the case added at landing; `make perf-check` 13 tests, 207 assertions. One migration, `Version20261101190536`, no Composer or npm package, no Makefile target, no cron. Working notes: **Built in #30** in `.claude/rules/module-crm.md`.

- **Schema:** `crm_email_templates` (`name` unique and case-sensitive, `subject`, `body_markdown`; `chk_crm_email_templates_content`: nothing blank), entity `App\Crm\Entity\EmailTemplate`, `EmailTemplateRepository`. Type `email_sent` takes position 7, so position 8 is the next free one. A sent mail is that activity: `title` the subject, `body` the text, payload `{to, cc?, status}`. `down()` deletes the `email_sent` entries.
- **Send:** `admin_api_crm_email_send` `POST /admin/api/crm/emails` (`App\Crm\Controller\Api\EmailsController`, `ROLE_EDITOR`, CSRF header) → `CrmApi::newEmail()` and `Form\NewEmail` → audited `Command\SendCrmEmail` → `CommandHandler\SendCrmEmailHandler`, which locks the record, sends and writes the entry. A 201 with `{entry}` answers `sent` and `failed` alike. A transport, RFC or mail-pipeline failure is an outcome: the entry stays `failed`, staff see it at once and keep their text; anything else bubbles and stores nothing. No address on the record is `NoEmailAddress`, a 422 on `contactId`.
- **The mail:** `App\Crm\Mailer\CrmMailer::send()`: `MAILER_FROM`'s address with the staff member's name, `Reply-To` `%site.contact_email%` (`hallo@`). The text part is the Markdown as written; the HTML is `templates/admin/crm/email/message.html.twig` on `emails/layout.html.twig`, with the body in the new macro **`rich(html)`** of `templates/emails/_blocks.html.twig`, which prints raw and takes `MarkdownRenderer` output only.
- **Override:** `INQUIRY_RECIPIENT_OVERRIDE` redirects every CRM mail and drops its CC, with both originals in `X-Crm-Original-Recipient`; the entry keeps the real `to`. The texts around the variable (`.env`, `app:prod:check-env`, `docs/operations.md`, `deploy/env.local.example`, the rule notes) now name CRM mail too.
- **Merge:** `App\Crm\ValueObject\MergeFields`, `TOKENS` `{contact.first_name}`, `{company.name}`, `{venue.name}` in one `strtr`: never Twig, a missing value is empty, an unknown token stays visible. Filled at pick time by `admin_api_crm_email_draft` `GET /admin/api/crm/email-draft?kind&recordId&template&contact` (`Form\EmailDraftRequest`, `ReadModel\EmailMergeQuery::fields()`), which writes nothing, so a token typed in the composer stays literal.
- **Composer:** a tab in the timeline island, not an island of its own. `admin-ui/src/crm/TimelineEmailFields.vue` holds An, Vorlage, Betreff and Cc; `TimelineComposer.vue` holds the values and `lastDraft`, so a tab change keeps them. Props: `TimelineProps::of()` takes `EmailRecipient::ofCompany()` or `ofContact()` and gives the island `email`, null on a deal. **Two buttons** on a record with an address: Senden, and E-Mail erfassen as before; with no address it only logs. Both are `type="button"`, so Enter never sends (D5). A recipient change merges again while the text is untouched (D3), also after a tab change or a failed send.
- **Timeline:** `Extension\EmailSentActivityType` (priority 250, `ROLE_EDITOR`, not composable, so read-only), `TimelineEntry::$email`, `TimelineList.vue`'s `email` renderer, a line in `types.js`, an `email_sent` branch in `_timeline.html.twig`. A failed entry heads "Nicht gesendete E-Mail von X".
- **Template screen:** `admin_crm_email_templates`, `_new`, `_edit`, `_delete` under `/admin/crm/vorlagen` (`Controller\EmailTemplatesController`, `ROLE_ADMIN`), `templates/admin/crm/email_template/{index,new,edit,_form}.html.twig`, `Form\EmailTemplateType`, `EmailTemplateData`, audited `SaveEmailTemplate` and `DeleteEmailTemplate`, reads through `ReadModel\EmailTemplateQuery`. Sidebar `Extension\EmailTemplatesNavigationItem`: Einstellungen, priority 100.
- **Stylesheets:** `assets/styles/crm/_timeline.scss` (`.crm-composer__email`, `.crm-composer__actions`, `.crm-timeline__pill`, `.crm-timeline__filters` wraps from 1024 px), `assets/styles/admin/_button.scss` (`.btn[aria-busy='true']`, which also reaches the other buttons that set it). **Catalogues:** `crm`: `timeline.email.*`, `email_template.*`, `api.no_email_address`, `nav.email_templates`, `activity.type.email_sent`, `activity.tab.email_sent`; `activity.tab.email_logged` is now "Erfasste E-Mails". `validators`: `crm.email.subject_line`. **Demo data:** `CrmStory` writes «Verlängerung Tier» and «Fotos anfordern» through `src/Factory/EmailTemplateFactory.php`.
- **Budgets:** one statement on a record page, `EmailTemplateQuery::options()`, only with a recipient. Test pins company 8, contact 6, deal 6; in production 9, 7, 7, under the ceilings of 10 and 9, none moved. Timeline island 10,775 of 30,720 B, admin CSS 14,229 of 30,720 B, before the rebase.
- **For later cycles:**
  - #40: `rich` is the only addition to `_blocks.html.twig`, so its restyling reaches the mail. Keep the macro's name and its raw print.
  - A new merge field is an entry in `MergeFields::TOKENS`, a constructor value and a read in `EmailMergeQuery`. Never write a `{token}` in a `+intl-icu` catalogue; `email_template.form.fields_help` takes them as a parameter.
  - A dead-SMTP test sets the DSN before the client boots: copy `EmailApiDeliveryFailureTest`.
  - The questionnaire mail in Out of scope has no bulk path here: `CrmMailer::send()` is one recipient per call, in the request.
- **Deviations:**
  - D1: two filter tabs, "Erfasste E-Mails" and "Gesendete E-Mails", against the design's one "E-Mails" (`Company.dc.html:94`). The bar holds eight tabs, so it wraps to two rows from 1024 px; below that it scrolls.
  - D6: pills say "Gesendet" and "Nicht gesendet", not the design's "Zugestellt": SMTP acceptance is not delivery, and nothing reads a bounce.
  - D7: no merge-field hint in the composer, as fields fill at pick time; the template screen shows them. No Markdown preview either.
  - D9: no stale check on the template form, against S12: at most two staff, never on the same thing.
  - Smaller than §30: no Mailer message id, no error column, no `queued`, no template merge-fields column.
  - The two composer buttons (above); the design and §30 show one send button.
  - The entry's shape is the plan's plain text, An, Cc, subject, whole body, against the design's head, subject and excerpt (`Company.dc.html:130-134`).
  - The sidebar icon is `mail`, the design's envelope, not the plan's `file-text`.
  - `phpunit.dist.xml` sets `memory_limit=512M` (test only). Peak 267 MB in the re-review's gate, 269 MB in the run before it on the same code, over the 256M of `docker/php/php.ini`: a pass had depended on a batch command test raising the limit, so on test order. Nothing enforces a figure.
  - #29's `CompanyRecordTest::test_an_inquiry_entry_reaches_the_island_and_the_fallback` no longer needs towns left by an earlier test (lead's call).
- **Decided, left as they are:**
  - Send before commit: SMTP runs inside the handler's transaction, so a database fault at the commit after SMTP accepted leaves a sent mail with no entry and a 500. Accepted: staff hold the text and may send again.
  - F-m5's `info` line naming the entry id is written, but production drops it: no Monolog, and the default `Logger` on stderr starts at ERROR. Accepted, an `error` per sent mail would be noise.
  - F-m8 left: the entry's subject has no weight of its own against the design. It sits in one joined plain string, so a weight needs its own element in `TimelineList.vue` and the fallback.
  - D3, **the owner should know:** a typed edit stops the merge, with or without a tab change. One typed letter, and A's name stays under a new recipient B with no sign in the composer. What is on screen goes out; it is the one way left to send one person's name to another.
  - D10: a race on the template name's unique key is a 500, at this scale.
- **Known, no action:** an anonymised failed entry still heads "E-Mail gesendet", having no payload and no pill. A known token with no value goes out as a gap, which staff see first. A deal record shows an always-empty "Gesendete E-Mails" tab. F-m7's second case, `edit` with an unknown id, has no test.
- **Stays manual for the owner:** Firefox, Safari, a real phone, a real mail client, and a person looking at the screens against the design and walking them by keyboard. Everything so far ran on headless Chromium and Mailpit.
- **Production check: open, after #20 and #30 are live.** The owner's, not a gate, and it needs DKIM. Send one mail from a CRM contact holding the owner's address and read `Authentication-Results`. Pass: SPF, DKIM and DMARC pass, From shows the staff name at `hallo@`, Reply-To is `hallo@`. Record date and result here.

## Location-Konten (W11–W13)

Location managers register, claim or create their location and keep it up to date themselves (S16, POR-1–POR-14). Read S16 first; its golden rules decide every open question in these cycles. #42 comes first, #43 and #44 run in parallel, and #45 comes last. Each deploys through #20's workflow, and every migration must be safe on production data. A cycle that wants a new Composer or npm package, a new wizard table or a new island needs a one-line reason in its research.

### 42. `portal-accounts`

W11. Depends on #23. No location screens yet: after this cycle a manager can register, log in and see an empty account. It also lays the access table and the voter, so #43 and #44 can run in parallel.

- **Module:** `Portal` in `ArchitectureRules::MODULES`, `ModuleBoundariesTest` and its probe; `.claude/rules/module-portal.md`; a row in `CLAUDE.md`'s module tables. `Crm` may depend on `Portal`; nothing else does (S2).
- **Schema:** `portal_users` (`id`, `email` lower-case and unique, `password_hash` null until the mail's link sets it, `first_name`, `last_name`, `phone`, `email_verified_at`, `is_active`, `last_login_at`, `created_at`, `updated_at`). `admin_audit_log` gains `portal_user_id` (FK, `ON DELETE SET NULL`), and `chk_admin_audit_log_actor_type` gains `portal_user`. `portal_venue_access` (`portal_user_id`, `venue_id`, `status` pending|active, `requested_at`, `confirmed_at`; primary key on both ids, both FKs `CASCADE`), so a user may hold many locations and a location may have many managers (POR-6), with its entity and a Foundry factory.
- **Access check:** `PortalVenueVoter` is the one place that decides whether a manager may touch a venue. Only `active` counts. Every later route with `{number}` uses it.
- **Firewall:** `portal` on `^/konto(/|$)`, one design-change edit of `security.yaml` (hotspot table), with form login, `login_throttling` (5 per 15 minutes), logout, CSRF and no 2FA. `PortalIdentity`, `PortalUserProvider`, `PortalArea` (asks the firewall map, like `AdminArea`, for the same reason). The hasher is `auto`.
- **Session cookie, the one trap:** `framework.session.cookie_path` is `/admin` for the whole app, so a browser would never send a session cookie to `/konto`. Decorate the session storage factory so that a request in the `portal` firewall gets path `/konto` and its own cookie name, and every other request stays exactly as it is. Do not widen the path to `/`: public pages must stay structurally free of a session (S10).
- **Pages** (all unlocalized, names `portal_*`, in `templates/portal/`): `/konto/registrieren`, `/konto/anmelden`, `/konto/passwort-vergessen`, `/konto/passwort-neu/{token}`, `/konto` (overview with an empty state) and `/konto/profil` (name, phone, password; a change of e-mail goes through staff).
- **Mails and tokens:** registration and password reset on `templates/emails/layout.html.twig`. Both open `/konto/passwort-neu/{token}`. Links are signed and expiring, with no token table, 48 hours from registration and one hour from the forgot form: the signature includes the password hash, so a used link dies by itself and every link dies once a password is set.
- **Abuse, cheaply:** #14's `website` honeypot, limiter `portal_register` per IP, and the same answer for a known and an unknown e-mail on registration and on the reset form. An account with no password gets the ordinary invalid-login answer. No external password check.
- **Look (S16):** `templates/portal/base.html.twig` and `base_auth.html.twig` copy the back office's shell and load `admin.css`, the `--a-*` tokens, `templates/admin/components/*` and the form theme. The sidebar holds "Meine Locations", "Profil" and "Abmelden", with no search, no extension points and no Admin Twig function that reads staff data.
