- **Guards `AdminArea` gives the back office and `/konto` needs too:** `Cache-Control: no-store, private` and `X-Robots-Tag: noindex, nofollow` on every response; `CleanText` on every text field (today `AdminTextConstraintExtension` covers the back office only, so a NUL byte in a `/konto` form would be a 500); `/konto` in `RobotsController::DISALLOW`.
- **Audit:** `AuditLogMiddleware` knows only `AdminIdentity`, so a manager's write would be logged as `system`. Add a `Shared` interface that `PortalIdentity` implements, the case `AuditActorType::PortalUser`, and show the manager's name in the audit log and the CRM timeline.
- **Staff:** `/admin/konten` (names `admin_portal_accounts*`, `NavigationItem` beside "Benutzer"): list and search, deactivate and reactivate (`ROLE_ADMIN`, audited). A deactivated account ends its sessions.
- **Public site:** "Location eintragen" in the header and the footer becomes a link to `/konto/registrieren`, which ends Q19's visual-only state. `templates/layout/*` belong to the redesign lanes, so this is the cycle's last step, a one-line change on top of what `main` then holds. `legal.privacy` says "Nur dort gibt es eine Anmeldung", which stops being true: narrow that sentence, nothing more (see the owner's rule on privacy work).
- **Done when:**
  - application tests cover register, the mail's link, login, wrong password, throttling, accounts with no password and deactivated accounts, reset (a used and an expired link), identical answers for known and unknown e-mails, and the honeypot;
  - a route walker like `ChecksAdminRoutes` proves every `/konto` route is closed to anonymous users, canonical and percent-encoded;
  - tests prove a manager cannot reach `/admin`, staff cannot reach `/konto`, both sessions live side by side in one browser, and a public page sets no cookie;
  - an integration test proves a manager's command is audited as `portal_user`, and the voter is tested for no row, `pending` and `active`;
  - S11's checks pass, and the screens match the back office at 1440 and 390 px.

**Outcome (2026-11-02).** Landed as one commit, `portal-accounts`, on the branch `portal`. Review: Needs Rework first (2 major, 5 minor), `Complete` on the re-review by a different reviewer (0 critical, 0 major, 10 minor, listed under Carried forward). A manager can register with name and e-mail, choose a password through the mail's link, log in, reset a password and keep a profile; staff can list, search, deactivate and reactivate accounts. There is no location screen yet: the access table and the voter are laid for #43 and #44. No new Composer or npm package, no cron, no new Makefile target (one parameter, below). Working notes: `.claude/rules/module-portal.md`.

- **Schema:** one migration, `Version20261101212259`, with `down()`, mapping `orm.mappings.Portal`. Tables `portal_users`, with `password_hash` nullable, and `portal_venue_access`; `admin_audit_log` gains `portal_user_id` (`ON DELETE SET NULL`), `chk_admin_audit_log_portal_user`, and `portal_user` in `chk_admin_audit_log_actor_type`. `portal_venue_access` keeps `created_at` and `updated_at` beside the spec's columns (`database.md`, every table). `phone` is `VARCHAR(40)`, the CRM's length.
- **Pages:** `portal_login` `/konto/anmelden`, `portal_register` `/konto/registrieren`, `portal_forgot_password` `/konto/passwort-vergessen`, `portal_reset_password` `/konto/passwort-neu/{token}`, `portal_home` `/konto`, `portal_profile` `/konto/profil`, POST `_logout_portal` `/konto/abmelden`. Staff: `admin_portal_accounts` `/admin/konten`, POST `admin_portal_accounts_{deactivate,reactivate}`, `ROLE_ADMIN`, audited, nav item "Konten" under Einstellungen.
- **Classes:** `App\Portal\Security\{PortalArea, PortalIdentity, PortalUserProvider, PortalPasswordHasher, PortalSessionStorageFactory, PortalLinkSigner, PortalVenueVoter, DevAutoLoginAuthenticator}`, `App\Portal\EventListener\{PortalResponseHeadersListener, RefusePortalLoginListener, RecordPortalLoginListener}`, `App\Portal\Entity\{PortalUser, PortalVenueAccess}`, `App\Portal\Mailer\PortalMailer`, `App\Portal\ReadModel\{PortalVenueAccessQuery, PortalProfileQuery, PortalAccountListQuery}`, `App\Portal\Form\PortalTextConstraintExtension`, `App\Shared\Security\PortalActor`. Commands: `RegisterPortalUser`, `RequestPortalPasswordReset`, `ResetPortalPassword`, `RecordPortalLogin`, and the audited `UpdatePortalProfile`, `ChangePortalPassword`, `DeactivatePortalUser`, `ReactivatePortalUser`. Limiter `portal_register`, 5 per hour per IP, shared by register and forgot. `AuditActorType::PortalUser`; `AuditLogQuery` and `VenueHeaderQuery` show the manager's name, and the CRM timeline gets it through `AuditLogQuery::forSubjects()`. `/konto` in `RobotsController::DISALLOW`, and one sentence of `legal.privacy` narrowed.
- **Accepted deviations:** the storage factory sets only the cookie's name; `PortalResponseHeadersListener` moves the cookie to `/konto`, because the session listener writes the static `/admin`. The audit FK goes through `resolve_target_entities` and the Shared `PortalActor`, because Admin may not name Portal. `PublicPagesLoadNoAdminAssetsTest` exempts `templates/portal/`, which loads the back office's parts on purpose. `/konto` logout sets a new `KONTOSESSID` instead of a delete, same as `/admin`: the logout POST's CSRF step writes into the invalidated session, and both ids are dead, proved on real sessions (Step 2.13's line 5 and the test were corrected, no production code). A bad link answers on a notice template with 410 instead of a redirect with a flash, because a flash after a redirect does not survive `SameSite=Strict` from webmail; that is reasoned, curl shows only that the first response needs no cookie. The texts of the `portal` catalogue are the agents' wording, not the owner's.
- **Registration takes no password (Review's first major finding, the lead's decision):** as first built, a second registrant's password was dropped without a word, so whoever registered an address first held the account once its owner clicked the mail, and a mail gateway's pre-fetch of the GET confirmation did the same with no click. Now register asks name and e-mail only, and POR-1, S16 and the sections above say so. The mail's link opens `portal_reset_password`; saving there sets the password and `email_verified_at` in one step (`PortalUser::setPasswordFromMail()`), and a GET changes nothing. `password_hash` is nullable, edited in place in the unlanded migration. Same signature, `registrationToken()` 48 h, `resetToken()` 1 h. Removed: routes `portal_confirm` and `portal_resend_confirmation`, commands `ConfirmPortalUser` and `ResendPortalConfirmation`, the confirm signature, the `login.error.unconfirmed` refusal, the `confirm` and `resend` catalogue prefixes. Registering again on a known address with no password sends the mail again, keeping the first name. The forgotten-password form on a no-password account sends the reset mail: one hour, and its wording, "neues Passwort angefordert", is slightly off for a first password, left as is.
- **Review's other changes:** the separation tests now send one side's session id under the cookie name the other side reads, and assert that the session was read, because a refusal alone also passes when no cookie arrives. Both mails greet with a plain "Guten Tag", because a first name in the greeting let anyone send text to a stranger's inbox from our domain. `PortalRoutesAreClosedTest` samples `VenueNumber::REQUIREMENT`. `tests/Deploy/release-check.sh` requests `/konto/anmelden`.
- **Additions:** `PORTAL=1` on `make lighthouse` and `make validate-html`, with the dev-only `Portal\Security\DevAutoLoginAuthenticator` and the seeded `manager@hochzeitslocation.test`. `VenueListQuery` got `VenueHeaderQuery`'s editor join, a third last-editor reader that would have left #44's manager edits blank in the CMS list. `memory_limit=512M` in `phpunit.dist.xml`, test only, same as #30's branch: the first full suite died at 49 % at the 256M of `docker/php/php.ini`, in `ImageVariantGeneratorTest`, not on an assertion; peak afterwards 269 MB. For uncommitted work use `RELEASE_REF=WORKTREE make release-check`: plain `make release-check` builds `HEAD`, and passed here with no `src/Portal` in the tarball.
- **Gate, at landing:** `make lint` passes, `make admin-lint` with it; `make test` passes, PHPUnit 4,838 tests, 58,066 assertions (the assertion count moves between runs), fewer tests than before because the confirm and resend tests went; Vitest 399 tests in 29 files; after Review's rework: the curl script on real PHP sessions through nginx passes all 8 lines; `RELEASE_REF=WORKTREE make release-check` passes, `/konto/anmelden` included; `make validate-html` on `/konto/registrieren` and a real `/konto/passwort-neu/{token}` page, no errors. The re-review ran `make perf-check`, 13 tests, 207 assertions, and Lighthouse on `/konto/registrieren` and a real `/konto/passwort-neu/{token}` page, 100 / 100 / 100 / 54 on both. Lighthouse, performance / accessibility / best practices / SEO: 100 / 100 / 100 / 54 on `/konto/anmelden`, `/konto` and `/konto/profil` with `PORTAL=1`, `/admin/konten` with `ADMIN=1`. SEO 54 on `/konto/anmelden` and `/admin/konten` is `is-crawlable` and `meta-description`, what a private page gives; on the other pages the score is the same and the failed audits were not read. `make validate-html`: 7 of 7 pages, no errors. The 410 notice was not validated, because the target's `curl -f` stops on it; it is the same template as the 200 notice.
- **Browser, plainly:** Claude in Chrome was used on `/konto`, `/konto/profil`, `/konto/registrieren`, `/konto/passwort-vergessen` and `/admin/konten`. `resize_window` has no effect there (`innerWidth` stays 1728), so 390 px and exact 1440 px came from headless Chromium in the Lighthouse image, not from Chrome. Chrome's synthetic Return is unreliable, so the keyboard pass of login, profile and logout ran headless with real key events; register and the mail ran in Chrome, before registration lost its password. No horizontal scroll at 390 px on the pages measured, also before that change. The re-review then saw the register and password pages as they stand now: in Chrome the register page at 531 px, a real mail in Mailpit and the password page at 1440 px, and in headless Chromium exact 1440 and 390 px, the error states, the keyboard walk, setting the password, login, and the dead link. No overflow at 390 px. `/konto/passwort-vergessen` was measured at both widths and looked at, at Chrome's width. At 390 px the sidebar stacks above the content, as on `/admin/benutzer`; it stays. Pending a person: the screens against the back office, the keyboard walkthrough, and mail content and tone. Firefox, Safari and a real phone are not proved. Small differences left for Review: `/konto/profil`'s read-only e-mail looks editable, `/konto/registrieren` has no autofocus, `/admin/konten`'s breadcrumb reads "Konten" where `/admin/benutzer` has two levels, its last column has no "Aktionen" header and "Deaktivieren" is a dark button, and the empty-form errors are the validator's default.
- **Carried forward:** the "Location eintragen" href is the lead's, at the move to `main`; `tests/Application/Shared/BaseLayoutTest.php:114-126` changes with it, and Q19's visual-only state ends only with the href, so the Public site line above is not met by this commit (met by `portal-finish`). #44 adds its JSON `entry_point` and `access_denied_handler` lines to `security.yaml` as a named exception, and its `csrf-token` meta to `templates/portal/base.html.twig`; `admin/form/theme.html.twig:163`'s Markdown preview route and the combobox manifest are #44's too. A later open `/konto` route must join `OPEN_ROUTES` in `PortalRoutesAreClosedTest` and `access_control`. The walker does not catch a `PUBLIC_ACCESS` line on a page with `#[CurrentUser]`, because the attribute answers the same redirect. The audit actor filter cannot pick a manager: `AuditLogFilter::$actorId` is `actor_id` only. `phone` is 40. #45's end-to-end path has to POST a password to the mail's link before logging in. The re-review's minors, none of them a product defect: `test_a_later_reset_keeps_the_first_confirmation` cannot fail, because both dates fall in one second of a `TIMESTAMP(0)` column, and backdating the first fixes it; registering again could send the reset mail unnoticed; the no-cookie assertion in `test_opening_the_mails_link_alone_changes_nothing` needs an empty jar; "nothing else changes" on registering again is pinned for one field in one branch; three comments are stale (`rate_limiter.yaml:8`, `PortalLoginTest.php:15`, `PortalSessionCookieTest.php:15-16`); `PortalUser::isConfirmed()` has only tests as callers. Screens: after registering, a 6-second toast on the login form is the only sign of the mail, which suits #45's onboarding; the forms show the validator's default wording; "E-Mail" breaks across two lines in the register intro; the first-password page says "Neues Passwort", a key the profile form shares.

### 43. `portal-claims`

W12. Depends on #27b and #42. Parallel with #44. After this cycle a manager can ask for access to a location that already exists, and staff decide. This is the simplest possible claim (owner, 2026-11-01): one button, one queue, nothing automatic.

- **Ask:** `/konto/locations/suchen` finds a location by name and town. "Zugriff anfragen" writes a `pending` row in `portal_venue_access`, and the screen says the request is with us. Asking twice changes nothing. A location that already has a manager follows the same path.
- **Staff decide:** `/admin/pruefung` (names `admin_portal_review*`) with the tab "Zugriffsanfragen", a `NavigationItem` with a badge and a `DashboardWidget`, showing the account's name, e-mail and phone and the location. Approve sets `active`. Reject deletes the row, so the manager may ask again. Both mail the manager and are audited.
- **Managers on a location:** a `RecordPanel` on the CMS venue page lists them, removes one, and adds an existing account by e-mail.
- **CRM (POR-7):** on approve, `Portal\Event\LocationManagerConfirmed`, and a `Crm\EventListener` built like #29's `IngestInquiryListener`: it finds or creates the `venue_contact` by e-mail, creates the venue's company through `CreateCompanyForVenue` when there is none, and writes a timeline entry. A known e-mail keeps staff's values. A failure is logged and never reaches the manager or the approving staff member.
- **Done when:**
  - application tests cover the search, asking, asking twice, approve, reject, asking again, the queue's badge and widget, and the panel;
  - integration tests cover the listener: new contact, known contact, a venue with and without a company, and a failing CRM that does not fail the approve;
  - the dashboard's query ceiling is raised on purpose, with the reason in the Outcome;
  - S11's checks pass at 1440 and 390 px.

**Outcome (2026-11-02).** Landed as one commit, `portal-claims`, on the branch `portal`. Review: Complete on one condition, 1 major and 6 minor findings. The major is the D9 change below, made at landing; only the landing gate has checked it, no reviewer has read it. A manager asks for access to a location at `/konto/locations/suchen`, and staff decide at `/admin/pruefung`; approving links the manager into the CRM. No migration, no package, no new island, no CSS. Tables: none changed.

- **Routes:** `portal_claims_search` `/konto/locations/suchen`, POST `portal_claims_ask` `/konto/locations/zugriff`; `admin_portal_review` `/admin/pruefung`, POST `admin_portal_review_approve` `…/anfragen/{userId}/{number}/annehmen`, POST `admin_portal_review_reject` `…/ablehnen`, POST `admin_portal_review_manager_add` `/admin/pruefung/locations/{number}/manager`, POST `admin_portal_review_manager_remove` `…/manager/{userId}/entfernen`.
- **Classes:** `App\Portal\Command\{RequestVenueAccess, GrantVenueAccess, RevokeVenueAccess}` and their handlers; `App\Portal\Controller\{ClaimController, ReviewController, VenueManagersController}`; `App\Portal\Event\{LocationManagerConfirmed, PortalEvents}`; `App\Portal\Exception\VenueNotClaimable`; `App\Portal\Extension\{AccessRequestsNavigationItem, AccessRequestsWidget, VenueManagersPanel}`; `App\Portal\ReadModel\{ManagerAccessQuery, PortalAccessRequestBadge, PortalAccessRequestQuery, PortalAccessRequestRow, VenueManagersQuery, VenueManagerRow}`; `App\Portal\Repository\PortalVenueAccessRepository`; `PortalMailer::{sendAccessApproved, sendAccessRejected}`; `App\Crm\Command\LinkLocationManager` and its handler, `App\Crm\EventListener\LinkLocationManagerListener`. `Cms\ReadModel\VenueListQuery::quickSearch()` takes an optional status.
- **Query ceilings, raised on purpose:** the dashboard rises from 10 to 12 in production terms with #43's badge and widget, and to 13 with #44's widget. The badge adds one statement to every back-office page, because it has no statement to ride on: `Crm` may not read a `Portal` read model. One statement per back-office page is fine at two staff users. So the company list, at 6 against its ceiling of 5, and the deal list, at 7 against its ceiling of 6, are past their ceilings, and the company record is at its ceiling of 10. The rest stay inside theirs, the contact record at 8 of 9.
- **D9, changed at landing (F-M1):** D9 kept a known contact's kind. A `couple` contact is the inquiry ingest's kind, not staff's, and `app:crm:purge` would have erased a confirmed manager 12 months on. Now `LinkLocationManagerHandler::contactOf()` makes a `couple` a `venue_contact`; other kinds and the fill rules stay as they were.
- **Other deviations:** `AddManagerData` and `AddManagerType` are not built, the panel reuses `PortalEmailData` and `PortalEmailType`. Approve and reject share one form through `formaction`. `/konto/locations/suchen` is one column with the action under the name at every width, because the action must be reachable at 390 px. The queue's long-row clip at 1440 stays, by the lead's decision. `venueName` is in the event's payload. All new keys sit under `claims` and `review`, the mail's under `claims.mail`. D8's warning is an `error` flash, because toasts have two kinds. The failing CRM is tested with a real constraint failure, `ReviewQueueTest::test_a_failing_crm_does_not_fail_the_approve` and its namesake in `LinkLocationManagerTest`.
- **Known, carried as they are (minor findings):** F-m1, the reject path's failed-mail branch has no test. F-m2, no kept test reads a mail body; the mails were read in Mailpit. F-m3, the queue's `via` is not pinned at the controller. F-m4, `ManagerAccessQuery`, `PortalAccessRequestQuery`, `VenueManagersQuery` and `PortalAccessRequestBadge` have no integration test of their own, and `quickSearch()`'s `$status` is absent from `VenueListQueryTest`; application tests reach all of them. F-m6, staff cannot see that an account is deactivated in the queue or the panel; deactivation still holds. F-m5 is closed: `module-crm.md` and `module-cms.md` carry their notes.
- **Not done:** the keyboard walk through approve, reject, add and remove with real presses, a real keyboard ask at 390 px, and a real phone. S11's comparison is partial: the queue was read beside `Inbox.png`, the widget and panel were not compared with `Main.png` or `LocationEdit.png`. A null-actor entry in the company timeline was read from the templates, not seen in a browser. All stay with the owner.
- **Carried forward:** no backfill for a failed CRM link, the cure is remove and add on the panel. No limit on asks per account, no paging on the queue. `/konto`'s list came with #44; #43 adds only the "Location suchen" button. #45 extends the listener through its private `link()`. `PublicCopyAddressTest::CATALOGUES` lacks `portal`, from #42.

### 44. `portal-editor`

W12. Depends on #25 and #42. Parallel with #43. After this cycle a manager maintains an existing location without staff. This is the first point where the owner saves data entry.

- **Overview:** "Meine Locations" lists the manager's locations with status and missing items (`VenueListRow::$missing`'s rules), and leads to `/konto/locations/{number}`.
- **Cards:** the Cms card forms and data classes as they are (`texts`, `address`, `terms`, `wedding`, `contact`, `faq`), each dispatching its existing `Venues` command through `VenueEditing`, with the existing stale check. `Portal` gets its own town lookup route, because the forms take `town_lookup_url` as an option and `/admin/api/cms/towns` is staff-only.
- **Not reachable (POR-8):** name, slug, tier, rating, reviews, rooms, documents, delete. The `basics` card carries tier and slug, so it is not reused: a small `ChangeVenueCapacity` command serves the one field a manager needs from it.
- **Photos:** the existing photo-manager island, which takes its URLs as props, on thin `/konto/api/locations/{number}/photos…` controllers that dispatch the same commands and answer the same JSON. The `room` action is left out with rooms. A location holds at most 30 photos, a limit that refuses rather than handles. Reread the limits written for trusted staff (`MEDIA_MAX_PIXELS`, `MEDIA_MAX_PORTRAIT_RATIO`, 25 MB, `SafeClientName`) with strangers in mind, and lower one only where it is cheap.
- **Preview:** `/konto/locations/{number}/vorschau` through `VenueDetailQuery::detail(includeUnpublished: true)`, like CMS-9.
- **Offline:** "Offline nehmen" dispatches `UnpublishVenue`. Going back online goes through the review, every time (#45), so a location staff took offline cannot switch itself back on, and no flag is needed.
- **Staff see changes (POR-9):** edits on a published location go live at once. The widget "Änderungen von Locations" reads `admin_audit_log` for `actor_type = 'portal_user'`, and the audit log gets that filter. No mail per edit.
- **Done when:**
  - a walker proves every `{number}` route and method answers 403 for another manager's location, and pending access counts as no access;
  - a test proves a crafted POST cannot change name, slug or tier;
  - a test per card proves the import guard holds (`cms_edited_at` is set) and the public page is purged;
  - tests cover the stale edit, the photo limit, a bad file, CSRF on every JSON write, and the unpublish;
  - `make admin-build` needs no island change beyond its props; a change there gets a Vitest case;
  - S11's checks pass at 1440 and 390 px.

**Outcome (2026-11-02).** Landed as one commit, `portal-editor`, on the branch `portal`. Review: Needs Rework first (1 major, 10 minor); the major was fixed and a fresh agent rechecked it over HTTP. A manager maintains an existing location at `/konto`: the overview lists the locations with status and missing items, seven cards, Fotos, Vorschau and "Offline nehmen". Every write is an existing `Venues` command, plus the new `ChangeVenueCapacity`, so the audit, the import guard and the purge come with it. Staff see the changes in the widget "Änderungen von Locations" and in the audit log's new actor type filter. No migration, no package, no new island. Working notes: `.claude/rules/module-portal.md`.

- **Routes:** `portal_location` `/konto/locations/{number}`, POST `portal_location_{texts,address,terms,wedding,contact,faq,capacity}`, `portal_location_photos` `…/fotos`, `portal_location_preview` `…/vorschau`, POST `portal_location_offline` `…/offline`; JSON `portal_api_photos_{add,order,alt,remove}` under `/konto/api/locations/{number}/photos`, `portal_api_towns` `/konto/api/towns`. Every `{number}` route carries `VenueNumber::REQUIREMENT` and `PortalVenueVoter::EDIT`.
- **Classes:** `App\Venues\Command\ChangeVenueCapacity` and its handler; `App\Portal\ValueObject\LocationCard`, `App\Portal\Form\{LocationForms, VenueCapacityType, VenueCapacityData}`, `App\Portal\Security\{PortalEntryPoint, PortalAccessDeniedHandler}`, `App\Portal\Controller\{LocationEditController, LocationPhotosController, LocationPreviewController, LocationOfflineController}`, `App\Portal\Controller\Api\{TownLookupController, PhotoUploadController, PhotosController}`, `App\Portal\ReadModel\{LocationChangesQuery, LocationChangeRow}`, `App\Portal\Extension\LocationChangesWidget`.
- **Extractions from Cms, the code moved unchanged, the existing tests unedited:** `Cms\Form\VenueCardSaver` with `Cms\ValueObject\VenueCardSaveOutcome`, `Cms\VenuePhotoUpload`, `Cms\TownOptions`, `templates/admin/cms/venue/_photo_manager.html.twig`, `VenueCard::editRoute()` for `_card_shell`, `back_url` on the preview template, `VenueListQuery::byNumbers()`. The island's one change is `v-if="endpoints.room"`, with its Vitest case.
- **`security.yaml` exception:** the two lines `entry_point` and `access_denied_handler` under `firewalls.portal`, the lead's named exception to the hotspot table, because a bad CSRF token or an anonymous JSON call would otherwise be a redirect to the login. The hotspot table's row at line 62 still names only #42.
- **Staff-visible changes, both planned:** the 30-photo limit (research D1) binds staff in the gallery and in each room, not the import, and its message says "Eine Location" for a room too. The JPEG scan refusal binds staff as well.
- **Lead's decisions, plainly:** A manager may empty their own published gallery, and the location stays published, because staff see it in the widget. A display name may pose as staff on the audit screen, because the stored actor is unforgeable and only two staff read that screen. `alt` and `remove` accept a room photo's id on a manager's own location by a hand-made request: own location, own photo, no refusal added. A venue deleted mid-request stays a 500 for that request. `portal_upload` (60 per hour per manager) was the lead's addition; it answers a JSON 422 on `file`, not a 429, because `upload.js` shows a text only for a 422. A staff minimum above a lowered maximum is cleared, with no message.
- **Other deviations:** the widget filters `subject_type = 'venue'` as well, because profile and password writes are audited on `portal_user`, and `v.status = 'published'`, so a location that went offline, the unpublish included, shows in the audit log only. The audit filter has its own labels, because `audit.actor.portal_user` reads "Gelöschtes Konto". The overview reads `PortalVenueAccessQuery::activeNumbers()` plus `byNumbers()`. No limit in `Media` was lowered, because a lower `MEDIA_MAX_PIXELS` would not bound decode time. Review found the cheap limit: `VenuePhotoUpload::add()` refuses a JPEG with more than 100 scan markers, staff included (F-M1). The dashboard's ceilings moved to 10 and 11, one statement for the new widget. Found in Phase I: the tabs used `_tabs`, whose bottom line stood 1 px above the header's; they now use `_record_tabs`, like every record page.
- **Gate at landing, after the rebase on `0d61e82`:** `make cs-fix`, fixed 0 of 1,549. `make lint` passes. `make test` passes, PHPUnit 5,112 tests, 61,665 assertions, Vitest 431 tests in 30 files. The rebase had one conflict, a line in `validators+intl-icu.de.yaml`, resolved to #40's wording.
- **Gate, Phase I:** `make cs-fix`, fixed 0 of 1,497. `make lint` passes, PHPStan and phpat included. `make test` passes, PHPUnit 4,918 tests, 59,218 assertions, Vitest 400 tests in 29 files. `make perf-check` passes, 13 tests, 207 assertions, no budget raised. After the tab fix and one comment: `make lint` again and PHPUnit `--filter 'Location|Portal|Dashboard'`, 277 tests, 2,509 assertions, not the whole suite. The curl check on real sessions through nginx passes all 6 lines: real token 200, wrong and missing token a JSON 403, the first session token on `/konto`.
- **S11, headless Chromium only:** exact 1440 and 390 px, the lighthouse tools image. `/konto`, `/konto/locations/1`, `…/fotos`, `…/vorschau`, `/admin`, `/admin/audit-log?actor_type=portal_user`, and `/admin/locations/1` for comparison: 14 page loads, all 200, no horizontal scroll, no console entry, no 4xx, no CSP report. Real login, then at both widths: the town combobox through `/konto/api/towns`, saved and shown again; an upload of 3.3 and 2.1 MB through nginx, 201; a text file refused with its message; a file over 10 MB refused in the browser; a keyboard move, with focus kept, announced and persisted; the alt text saved and read back, no room field. The offline modal by keyboard: Enter opens it, focus on "Abbrechen", Escape returns it to the trigger, fitting at 390 (356 px); the confirm at 1440 turned the pill to "Unpubliziert" and removed the trigger. The unsaved guard fired. Only console entry in the flow: the expected 422. Lighthouse, logged in with `PORTAL=1`: `/konto/locations/1` 100 / 100 / 100 / 54, `/konto` 97 / 100 / 100 / 54, `…/fotos` 96 / 100 / 100 / 58, Accessibility 100 on each, SEO low because of `noindex`. `make validate-html`: no errors on `/konto`, `/konto/locations/1`, `…/fotos` and `/admin/audit-log`, filtered too.
- **Not done:** a real browser. Neither Implement nor Review ran the keyboard walkthrough in Claude in Chrome or a real file from disk at 390 px; headless stopped at 45 stops in Terms, so 4 of 7 cards, the overview and 390 px were not walked. Both stay with the owner, as do `RELEASE_REF=WORKTREE make release-check` and prod mode. No screenshot has been judged against the back office by anyone but the implementer.
- **Carried forward:** going back online, "Wieder veröffentlichen" and `/einrichten/{step}` are #45's; `{step}` must be taught to the walkers. `MoveVenuePhotoToRoom`, staff only, can still lift a scope over 30. The JSON 401's body reads "Du" from `admin`, which the island never shows. The photo panel's hint says "Alt-Text und Zuordnung" to a manager who has no room field. Review's minors, none a product defect: a lost race at the limit leaves an original on disk, and no sweep covers `MEDIA_ROOT/cms` (`AddVenuePhotoHandler.php:76,84`); lowering the maximum clears staff's minimum and the audit row shows only `capacityMax`; no test asserts `cms_edited_at` on the `/konto` photo routes or on `RemoveVenuePhoto`, nor the manager in the audit of photo writes; `activeNumbers()` has no integration test; the hotspot `admin+intl-icu.de.yaml:125-132` has no named exception; plan P8's reason is false, `.widget__head-link` exists; `VenuePhotoUpload::rule()` being public and the `MAX_SIZE` alias have no caller; a bomb stored before the refusal would decode again in `app:media:regenerate`, which has no time limit on the CLI. A JPEG carrying megabytes of embedded data can be refused falsely, accepted because the message says to re-save.

### 45. `portal-onboarding`

W13. Depends on #43 and #44. After this cycle anyone can put a new location on the site, and the owner only checks it.

- **Schema:** `portal_submissions` (`venue_id` as primary key, `CASCADE`; `status` draft|submitted|rejected|accepted; `submitted_by`, `submitted_at`, `reviewed_by`, `reviewed_at`, `reject_reason`, `rights_confirmed_at`). `venues.status` is unchanged: a location under review is `unpublished`, so no public query changes.
- **Way in:** a manager with no location lands in the wizard straight after the first login. The wizard opens with the name, shows possible matches and offers "Zugriff anfragen" (#43), which is what prevents duplicates.
- **Wizard (POR-11):** short server pages at `/konto/locations/{number}/einrichten/{step}`, POST → redirect → GET, one progress bar:
  1. Name, maximum guests, PLZ / Ort: `CreateVenue` with `Tier::Basic`, then `RelocateVenue`.
  2. Texte: `ChangeVenueTexts`.
  3. Ausstattung: `ChangeVenueTerms`, `ChangeVenueWeddingFacts`.
  4. Fotos: the #44 island; a form fallback is enough for the first upload.
  5. Kontakt: `ChangeVenueContact`.
  6. Prüfen: what is still missing, the preview, the rights checkbox, "Veröffentlichen".

  There is no wizard table: the current step is derived from what the location lacks, so a manager who leaves resumes in the right place, and every step reuses #44's forms.
- **"Veröffentlichen" (POR-12):** checks the same rule as `PublishVenue` (a shared query, not a copy), stores the rights confirmation, and sets `submitted`. It fires `Portal\Event\LocationSubmitted`, on which #43's listener creates the contact and the company, mails the manager and notifies `site.contact_email`. The page then says "Wird geprüft". Editing stays open during the review.
- **Review (POR-13):** the tab "Neue Locations" in #43's queue, with the preview. Accept dispatches `PublishVenue` and mails the manager. Reject takes a reason, mails it, and returns the location to the wizard's last step for another try. The queue reads only unpublished locations, so a publish straight from the CMS empties it with no extra code. Spam goes through the existing `DeleteVenue`.
- **Back online:** "Wieder veröffentlichen" (#44's rule) takes this same path.
- **Invoice (POR-14):** no invoice, payment or tier code. Q20 is answered "paid". The first time a newly created location goes live, one CRM task, "Rechnung stellen", is written on its company, due 30 days after that publish. "Newly created" means made through `CreateVenue`: the wizard or the CMS's "Neue Location". "Goes live" is an accept in the queue or a publish in the CMS, whichever comes first. A venue with no CRM company gets one first. Imported and seeded venues, a return from offline, a reject followed by an accept, a queue accept after a CMS publish and a claimed location write none. The fact is `venues.awaits_first_publish` (`portal-invoice-task`, migration `Version20261102070544`), set by `CreateVenue` and cleared by the first publish; `PublishVenue` answers it and its caller fires `Venues\Event\NewVenuePublished`, on which the CRM writes the task. A CRM failure is logged and never fails the publish.
- **Done when:**
  - application tests walk a new location from registration to a live public page, with the page absent (410) until the accept;
  - tests cover resuming at the derived step, the duplicate hint, a publish with something missing, reject and resubmit, going back online, and that a manager without access cannot touch a draft;
  - integration tests cover `LocationSubmitted` in the CRM, also when the CRM fails;
  - a new location appears in the listing, the sitemap and its canton page after the accept, purged like any CMS publish;
  - S11's checks pass at 1440 and 390 px, and the wizard is usable without JS except for the photo step's island.

**Outcome (2026-11-02).** Landed as one commit, `portal-onboarding`, on the branch `portal`. Review: `Complete` (0 critical, 0 major, 11 minor, listed under Carried forward). A manager can now put a new location on the site without staff help up to the last check. A manager with no location lands in a wizard after the first login: name, guests and place with a duplicate hint offering #43's "Zugriff anfragen", then Texte, Ausstattung, Hochzeit, Fotos, Kontakt and Prüfen. The bare URL derives the step from what the location lacks, so there is no wizard table, and a manager who leaves resumes there. "Veröffentlichen" checks the same rule as `PublishVenue`, stores the rights confirmation, mails the manager and `site.contact_email`, and fires `LocationSubmitted`, which writes the CRM company and contact. Editing stays open during the review. Staff accept or reject in the tab "Neue Locations" of #43's queue. An accept publishes the location. A reject mails its reason and returns the location to the check for another try. "Wieder veröffentlichen" after "Offline nehmen" takes the same path. No package, no new island, no cron, no config change, no new Makefile target. Q20 stayed at its default, free, in this cycle; answered "paid" on 2026-11-02, and `portal-invoice-task` added the CRM task, on the first publish of any newly created location, the CMS's included. Working notes: `.claude/rules/module-portal.md`.

- **Schema:** one migration, `Version20261102020630`, with `down()`. Table `portal_submissions`, key `venue_id`, FK `CASCADE`; `status` draft|submitted|rejected|accepted, `chk_portal_submissions_status`; `submitted_by` to `portal_users`, `SET NULL`; `reviewed_by` to `admin_users`, `RESTRICT`; `submitted_at`, `rights_confirmed_at`, `reviewed_at`, `reject_reason` `VARCHAR(1000)`, `created_at`, `updated_at`; three indexes. `venues.status` is unchanged, so no public query changed.
- **Pages:** `portal_locations_new` GET, POST `/konto/locations/neu`, the only step before a number exists. `portal_location_setup` GET `/konto/locations/{number}/einrichten`, `portal_location_setup_step` GET, POST `…/einrichten/{step}`, steps `texte`, `ausstattung`, `hochzeit`, `fotos`, `kontakt`, `pruefen`, behind `PortalVenueVoter::EDIT`; a published location redirects to `portal_location`. Staff: POST `admin_portal_review_submission_accept` `/admin/pruefung/neu/{number}/annehmen`, GET, POST `admin_portal_review_submission_reject` `…/ablehnen`, the tab `?tab=neu` of `admin_portal_review`. `/konto` redirects a manager with no access row, pending included, to `portal_locations_new`.
- **Classes:** `App\Portal\Entity\Submission`, `App\Portal\Repository\SubmissionRepository`, `App\Portal\ValueObject\{SubmissionStatus, WizardStep}`, `App\Portal\ReadModel\{SubmissionQuery, SubmissionState, PendingSubmissionQuery, PendingSubmissionRow}`, `App\Portal\Controller\{NewLocationController, WizardController, SubmissionReviewController}`, `App\Portal\Form\{NewLocationData, NewLocationType, WizardContactType, WizardForms, WizardPhotoType, SubmitLocationData, SubmitLocationType, RejectSubmissionData, RejectSubmissionType}`, `App\Portal\Event\LocationSubmitted`, `App\Portal\Exception\{TooManyUnfinishedLocations, SubmissionNotOpen}`, `App\Venues\ReadModel\VenuePublishGaps`, which holds `PublishVenueHandler`'s SQL, moved unchanged. Commands: `CreateOwnLocation`, `SubmitLocation`, `AcceptSubmission`, `RejectSubmission`. `PortalMailer` gains `sendLocationSubmitted()`, `sendLocationNotice()`, `sendLocationAccepted()`, `sendLocationRejected()`. `Crm\EventListener\LinkLocationManagerListener::onLocationSubmitted()` is the CRM side.
- **Accepted deviations:** the cap, at most 10 unfinished locations per account, is an overrule of the spec, one check in the handler. Ruled by the lead, not findings: `inquiry_email` required at step 5 by the wizard's form only, since `VenuePublishGaps` has no such gap; the staff queue's sideways scroll at 390 px; a stale `submitted` row, which a publish straight from the CMS leaves behind and which only drops out of the queue; #43's D9, below. `ausstattung` and `hochzeit` are two pages at step 3 of 6. After a save the wizard goes to the next step in a fixed order, because a derived step would jump over a filled one. D9 reaches #45 through the shared handler, so a `couple` who submits becomes a `venue_contact`; `test_a_couple_who_submits_becomes_a_venue_contact` pins it, added at the rebase. The fallback also checks the portrait and JPEG scan limits, through the new public `VenuePhotoUpload::isTooTall()`, `hasTooManyScans()` and `maxPortraitRatio()`; that file is outside the plan's list, and the checks moved unchanged. `reject` does not catch `SubmissionNotOpen`, by the two-staff rule. `sendAccessMail()` now calls `sendLocationMail()`. `location_submitted` has a button to `portal_home`. "Neue Location" is a secondary button, so the page keeps one primary action. `LocationRoutesAccessTest`'s `WALKED` went from 15 to 18 on purpose. The rest are in the changes log. The mails on "Veröffentlichen" go out from `WizardController::announce()`, in the shape of `SubmitInquiryController`, as the plan's step says, where the spec's text has #43's listener send them; the listener does the CRM.
- **Gate, at landing:** `make lint` passes, `make admin-lint` with it; `make test` passes, PHPUnit 5,357 tests, 64,456 assertions; Vitest 431 tests in 30 files; `make perf-check` passes, 14 tests, 216 assertions, all on the tree of this commit but for documents.
- **Browser, plainly:** headless Chromium only. Review did not use Claude in Chrome, and the changes log records no use of it. S11, from the changes log's Phase 8, ran on `fb33f50`, before the rebase, and was not rerun; `git diff fb33f50 43823a5` touches no template. There: exact 1440 and 390 px through nginx, 35 screens each on real sessions, the whole path from registration to accept, one real upload through the island, 201 at each width. No horizontal page scroll, 0 page errors, 0 failed requests, 0 CSP violations by a browser listener; the only 4xx were the 10 deliberate 422s. JS off at 390 px, from registration to "Wird geprüft", the first photo through the `<noscript>` form. The keyboard was a scripted Tab walk on step 1 with matches, `pruefen` and the start of the reject page, whose form it did not reach. Lighthouse from that same run, performance / accessibility / best practices / SEO: `/konto` 99 / 100 / 100 / 54, `/konto/locations/neu` and `…/63/einrichten/pruefen` 100 / 100 / 100 / 54, `/admin/pruefung?tab=neu` 100 / 100 / 100 / 54, `/admin/pruefung/neu/64/ablehnen` 98 / 100 / 100 / 54; SEO is low because of `noindex`. `make validate-html`: no errors on those screens, one warning, "Section lacks heading", on the reject page. Screens were compared with `LocationEdit.png` by the implementer only. Review's own walk on `43823a5`, also headless Chromium: the whole path with JS, without JS for the fallback, and a required-fields walk at 390 px with no overflow; 16 mails read and machine-scanned clean. Review did not rerun Lighthouse or HTML validation and did not compare 1440 and 390 px against the boards. Review lists as not run: real mail clients, a real webmail click, Safari, Firefox, a phone, the island's arrow and drag reorder, photo removal, prod mode, a real cache purge, spam delete from the queue, an accept on a staff-offline location.
- **Carried forward:** Review's minors, a short wording and test clean-up after the owner's first look. F-m1: the duplicate hint's heading sits only in the table's `aria-label`. F-m2: the refusal reads "Es fehlt: Haupt-Gebäudeart fehlt". F-m3: an Instagram handle publishes a dead link such as `https://@remiserebhalde`, a field older than #45. F-m4: without JS the working form sits under "braucht JavaScript". F-m5: the fallback's pixel refusal shows the framework's text; the refusal holds. F-m6: the same "new location" POST twice makes two drafts, which a manager cannot delete and which both count against the cap. F-m7: a second "Veröffentlichen" writes a no-op audit row. F-m8: step 1 says "Der Slug braucht…" to a manager who has no slug. F-m9: a docblock in `OnboardingOverviewTest` says the redirect is not followed, but the test follows it. F-m10: `CreateOwnLocationHandler`'s docblock repeats a rounding reason the changes log disproved, and `test_the_relocate_token_survives_a_rounded_second` does not bite. F-m11: `onboarding.step.*` is built at runtime with no test for `texte` or `kontakt`, and `onboarding.step.pruefen` is dead. All five manual rows of the plan stay pending human verification: 1440 px against `docs/reference/admin/`, every step at 390 px, the keyboard walk, a real browser with JS off, the four mails in Mailpit. For the lead's ruling: no dashboard widget lists new locations. Neither spec nor plan asks for one, the dashboard says "Keine offenen Zugriffsanfragen", and the staff mail and the sidebar badge do announce the location. Live at once is proved on the dev lane only, not behind a CDN: the purge at runtime was read, plus `OnboardingWalkTest.php:122`, and `http_cache` is prod-only. The "Location eintragen" href stays with the lead. ## Liechtenstein (W14, Q13)

### 46. `liechtenstein-venues`

W14. Depends on #3, #21 and #32. Q13 answered yes on 2026-11-02, so the 4 Liechtenstein venues in `Locations+Startseite.csv` go on the site. It blocks nothing and can land before or after go-live.

- **Region:** Liechtenstein becomes one region next to the 26 cantons, as a `li` row in `cantons`, so a venue there gets `canton_code = 'li'` and needs no new column. Research checks what assumes 26 rows or a Swiss BFS number: `bfs_number` (Liechtenstein has no Swiss canton number; pick a value outside 1–26 or make it nullable), the `Canton` enum and `canton.li` in the `geo` catalogue, region chips, the `canton=` filter, and the canton landing page with #17's threshold.
- **Towns:** the 11 Liechtenstein municipalities with their ZIPs (9485–9498) and coordinates in `data/geo/towns.csv` or a second file `app:geo:import-towns` reads, with the source in `data/geo/SOURCE.md`. The place search and the radius search work across the border with no change.
- **Map:** `assets/maps/cantons.svg` gains `ch-li` from the same open dataset, or the map leaves Liechtenstein out and the region works without a path. Research decides by size against S11's HTML budget; record the licence in `assets/maps/LICENSES.md`.
- **Import:** `LegacyVenueMapper` and the owner exporter stop skipping `Liechtenstein` and map it to `li`. `+423` phones go to E.164. A re-run of `app:legacy:export-content`, `app:owner:export-content` and `app:venues:import` adds the 4 venues and changes nothing else.
- **Done when:**
  - a re-run of the import on the real CSV reports 247 venues, 0 skipped, 0 unresolved, and a second run changes nothing;
  - the 4 detail pages answer 200, the listing filters them by the Liechtenstein region, and the place search finds Vaduz;
  - the landing page for Liechtenstein exists if #17's threshold allows it, and the sitemap follows;
  - the existing geo, search and SEO tests pass with 27 regions, and tests cover the `li` row, a `+423` phone and the import counts;
  - S11's checks pass on the pages it changes.

## Out of scope / proposals (not scheduled)

- **Location-Konten extensions:** rooms and documents in the account (the same thin-controller recipe as #44's photos), inquiries in the account, 2FA for managers, a change of e-mail by the manager, online payment and a tier upgrade by the manager (Q20 and the deal flow stay the way to sell tiers).
- **CRM extensions:** custom properties on companies, contacts and deals; inbox sync (IMAP/BCC address) to log replies; e-mail open/click tracking; reports beyond the dashboard widgets; invoicing and payment status; automatic tier downgrade when a contract ends (#28 only reminds).
- **Back office dark theme** and a mobile-first back office (S12 only requires that it stays usable at 390 px).
- **Map view ("Karte")** of the result list.
- **Bulk inquiry from the Merkliste** (eventlokale has it).
- **Facts for the other ≈640 venues:** a questionnaire mail to venues without owner data, sent through #30 and answered into the CMS (S14, **Coverage**).
- **A curated Ambiente vocabulary:** ~8 style codes (romantisch, modern, rustikal, …) as a filter, built from S14's 176 free texts.
- **Testimonials on the home page:** "Was unsere Paare sagen", once real, consented quotes exist; a content block, no new table.
- **Canton map on the home page:** the inline `{{ canton_map() }}` as links to the canton pages, if the HTML budget allows it.
- **Magazin and Partner pages:** both are in the graphic designer's navigation and remain out of scope.
- **Own category icons** from the graphic designer, replacing the Lucide stand-ins.
- **French, Italian and English** (one cycle per language: catalogues, localized route paths, venue translations, hreflang). S4 makes these additive.
- **A TypeScript/JS framework layer** (only if a real need appears).
- **Analytics** (the owner decided on no consent banner, so any analytics must be cookieless).
- Ratings collected on the site (we display imported ratings only), provider listings, magazine, Award, packages.
